# Kod va hujjat auditi — 2026-09-02

2026-08-26 auditi (2026-08-29 qayta tekshiruvi bilan) ning davomi, `origin/main` (`db2382a`, prod bilan bir xil) ustida. Bazadagi 768 topilma (715 eski + 53 ta 08-29 da qo'shilgan) hozirgi kod bilan qayta solishtirildi; `docs/` ↔ kod sync va butun manba sweep'i (2026-09-02 ertalab, eski daraxtda) natijalari shu bazaga qarshi qayta tekshirildi. Har topilma: adversarial rad etish + `grep -F` iqtibos darvozasi; yuqori jiddiylikdagilar repro bilan BAJARIB isbotlangan.

### `backend/app/api/routes/orders/lifecycle.py:425` — BUG · origin-08-29 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

Ish markazini PRODUCTION holatidagi buyurtmada qayta tanlash `order.machine_assignments` ni yangilaydi, lekin `PartRoute.machine_id` eski stanokda qolib ketadi — yangi stanokdagi skan E001 bilan rad etiladi.

**Dalil:** order.machine_assignments = {str(k): v for k, v in body.assignments.items()}

**Repro:** scratchpad/r3->r2.py: haqiqiy `L.assign_order_machines` ni soxta DB/Post/Machine bilan chaqirdim (_get_order/_require_editable/log_action patch). Natija: `order.machine_assignments = {'ARRA': 11}`, lekin `PartRoute.machine_id oldin=[(100,10),(101,10)] keyin=[(100,10),(101,10)]`, `ROUTE MUHRI YANGILANDIMI: False`, `db.add` chaqirilmagan. So'ng scanning.py:581 shartini o'sha satrlarga qo'llab: `E001: True`, terminal.py:302 filtri: `ko'rinadimi: False`.

**Tekshiruv:** assign_order_machines hali ham faqat order.machine_assignments ni yozadi; PartRoute.machine_id muhrlash sikli faqat start_order_production ichida (lifecycle.py:564-580). _EDITABLE_IF_UNTOUCHED (=_common.py:27) PRODUCTION'da skansiz buyurtmaga qayta tanlashga ruxsat beradi, natijada terminal.py:302 filtri yangi stanokdan yashiradi va scanning.py:583 E001 beradi. Tuzatish arzon: siklni helper'ga chiqarib assign'da ham chaqirish.

**Tuzatish:** `start_order_production` dagi stamping siklini (`lifecycle.py:469-486`) `_common.py` ga helper qilib chiqarib, `assign_order_machines` da ham chaqirish (buyurtma allaqachon PRODUCTION bo'lsa marshrut qatorlari qayta muhrlansin).

### `backend/app/api/routes/orders/matrix.py:200-203` — BUG · yangi sweep · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

When a matrix stage checkbox is toggled on for a post that has no existing PartRoute step yet, the new step is always created with per_instance=1. For KROMKA this is wrong: scanning.py explicitly requires per_instance to equal the edge count for KROMKA ('KROMKA qirralari' — scanning.py:605, and _set_custom_route in this same file computes per_instance=edge_count for KROMKA via `first_edge_pass`, matrix.py:311-323). A KROMKA step created through this stage-toggle path only requires 1 scan instead of edge_count scans.

**Dalil:** per_instance=1,                         total_required=part.count,

**Repro:** uv run python scratchpad/r36.py — matrix.edit_order_matrix ni _get_order/_require_editable/log_action/read_order_matrix patch qilib, fake db bilan chaqirdim: part(is_custom=True, edges=4 ta, count=2), KROMKA qadam yo'q, stages={3:True}. db.add ga tushgan PartRoute: per_instance=1 total_required=2; _set_custom_route/ingest.py:147-158 bo'lsa per_instance=4 total_required=8 bo'lardi (total_required ham `* per` siz). Backend custom detal uchun stages ni bloklamaydi; UI OrderMatrix.tsx:327 da qulflaydi, standart detalda edges bo'lsa AUTO KROMKA doim bor (ingest.py:390) — demak faqat to'g'ridan-to'g'ri API orqali. Sev medium; fix 2 qator, regress xavfi past.

**Tekshiruv:** Stage-toggle orqali yaratilgan yangi PartRoute hali qattiq per_instance=1 (va total_required=part.count, ko'paytiruvsiz) — KROMKA uchun edge_count hisoblanmaydi. _set_custom_route (370-qator atrofida) hamon boshqacha hisoblaydi. Asl baho (UI orqali qiyin erishiladi, sev pasaytirilsin) hali amal qiladi.

**Tuzatish:** Compute per_instance the same way _set_custom_route does (edge_count = len(part.edges) or 1, per_instance = edge_count when post.code == PostCode.KROMKA) before creating the new PartRoute in the stages loop, instead of hardcoding per_instance=1.

### `backend/app/api/routes/tmo.py:224-225` — BUG · yangi sweep · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

Prefix check is case-insensitive via .upper(), but the original non-uppercased `qr` is then used for the actual pachka lookup (`_pachka_by_code(db, order, qr)` at line 230, which does exact `Pachka.code == code`). Pachka codes are always generated uppercase (pachka.py:235: f"PK-{order.number}-{used+1:02d}"), so a lowercase/mixed-case scan is recognized as a pachka QR but then fails to be found.

**Dalil:** if qr.upper().startswith("PK-"):

**Repro:** uv run python scratchpad/r35.py — tmo._pachka_by_code fake AsyncSession bilan (PG varchar `=` case-sensitive emulyatsiyasi, Pachka.code String(40), citext/collation yo'q). Natija: 'PK-B-2026-001-01' prefix_ok=True -> found; 'pk-b-2026-001-01' prefix_ok=True -> HTTPException E001 "qadog'i topilmadi"; 'Pk-b-...' ham E001. Frontend useTerminalScan.ts:66 faqat trim(), toUpperCase yo'q; bridge_agents.py:445 PK- ni TMO'ga yetkazmay rad etadi — yo'l faqat terminalda qo'lda kiritish. Sev aslida medium.

**Tekshiruv:** Bug hali bor: prefiks testi qr.upper() bilan, lekin _pachka_by_code(db, order, qr) ga xom (uppercase qilinmagan) qr uzatiladi (225-qator), _pachka_by_code esa Pachka.code == code aniq solishtiradi (193-208). Kichkina qo'lda kiritilgan lowercase QR uchun hali ham muammo.

**Tuzatish:** Normalize once and reuse it: `qr_upper = qr.upper()`, use it both for the prefix test and pass it into `_pachka_by_code`.

### `backend/app/models/base.py:23` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`updated_at` is declared nullable with `default=None` AND `server_default=current_timestamp(0)`, so SQLAlchemy omits it from the INSERT and every freshly created row gets a non-NULL `updated_at` — it is never NULL, defeating the frontend's "never edited" check.

**Dalil:** server_default=text("current_timestamp(0)"),

**Repro:** Repro (r16_updated_at.py): TimestampMixin bilan minimal model, sqlite'da jadval qo'lda `updated_at DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP` bilan yaratildi, ORM orqali insert. Natija: `INSERT INTO thing (title, created_at) VALUES (?, ?) RETURNING id, updated_at` — `updated_at` INSERT ustunlar ro'yxatida YO'Q (SQLAlchemy None + server_default → ustun tashlab ketiladi), qayta o'qilganda `updated_at = 2026-09-02 04:23:19` (NULL emas). `Order.__table__.c.updated_at`: nullable=True, server_default='current_timestamp(0)'. created_at Python'dan mikrosekund bilan, updated_at serverdan (0) aniqlikda keladi → frontend `$orderId.tsx:365` `updated_at !== created_at` yangi buyurtmada ham true bo'ladi. Tuzatish migratsiya talab qiladi (barcha jadvallarda DROP DEFAULT).

**Tekshiruv:** updated_at hali nullable+default=None+server_default=current_timestamp(0): INSERTda ustun tushirib qoldiriladi, DB sekund aniqlikda to'ldiradi, created_at esa Python mikrosekund — frontend/src/routes/_layout/orders/$orderId.tsx:391 `order.updated_at !== order.created_at` doim true, yangi buyurtmada soxta 'Oxirgi tahrir'. Migratsiyalarda updated_at uchun DROP DEFAULT yo'q.

**Tuzatish:** Drop `server_default=text("current_timestamp(0)")` from `TimestampMixin.updated_at` and add a migration that does `ALTER TABLE <each> ALTER COLUMN updated_at DROP DEFAULT` (plus a one-off `UPDATE ... SET updated_at = NULL WHERE updated_at = created_at`-style backfill if the historical rows matter). Keep `onupdate` — that is what actually maintains the column.

### `backend/app/services/giblab/ingest.py:155` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

restore_custom_routes appends restored MANUAL steps AFTER the freshly-ingested tail (UPAKOVKA/TMO), so TMO is no longer the last step of a standard part's route.

**Dalil:** start_seq = max((r.seq for r in live), default=0) + 1

**Repro:** scratchpad/r3.py: haqiqiy `I.restore_custom_routes` ni soxta DB bilan chaqirdim. live (AUTO) = [(seq1,code1 ARRA),(seq2,code3 KROMKA),(seq3,code16 TMO)], saved MANUAL=[6 PRISADKA]. Natija: `TIKLANGAN: seq=4 process_code=6 source=manual`, `TMO seq=3 | tiklangan MANUAL seq=4`, `MANUAL DUMDAN KEYINGA TUSHDIMI: True`.

**Tekshiruv:** restore_custom_routes standart shoxida start_seq = max(live seq)+1 — live ichida ingest_project ning AUTO qadamlari bor va ular doim TMO (16) bilan tugaydi (ingest.py:403), tiklangan MANUAL qadam TMOdan keyin tushadi; scanning STRICT_STAGE_ORDER seq bo'yicha tekshirib E004 beradi. matrix.py:170-210 dagi tuzatish (not_in(TAIL_CODES) + dumni surish) bu yerga ko'chirilmagan.

**Tuzatish:** Dum qadamlarini (TAIL_CODES) ajratib, yangi MANUAL qadamlarni dumdan OLDIN qo'yish va dum `seq` larini surish — ikkala joyda (ingest.restore_custom_routes va orders.edit_order_matrix) bitta helper bilan.

### `backend/app/services/giblab/ingest.py:78` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

snapshot_custom_routes only sees parts that already have a MANUAL route row, so a part marked "Nostandart" (or bound to an assembly parent) before any route was drawn silently loses is_custom and assembly_parent_id on re-upload.

**Dalil:** .join(PartRoute, PartRoute.part_id == Part.id)

**Repro:** Repro (r18_snapshot.py): FakeDB 3 so'rovga navbat bilan javob beradi — keys=[(1,PROD,P1),(2,PROD,P2)], parents=[(1,2)] (P1 is_custom=True, P2 ga biriktirilgan), MANUAL part_route qatorlari=[] . `snapshot_custom_routes` → `{}`; 3-so'rov matni `JOIN part_route ON` INNER (LEFT OUTER emas) — tasdiqlandi. Xuddi shu holat bitta MANUAL qator bilan → `{('PROD','P1'): (True, [6], ('PROD','P2'))}`. `restore_custom_routes(saved={})` → `(0, [])` (erta qaytadi). Oyna haqiqiy: matrix.py:115-127 is_custom yoqilganda MANUAL yozmaydi, faqat AUTO soft-delete; frontend OrderMatrix.tsx:88 `is_custom` ni route_codes'siz yuboradi; biriktirilgan mayda detalga _set_custom_route dum ham qo'shmaydi (matrix.py:339 `if codes and ...`) — ya'ni bo'sh kodli nostandart/biriktirilgan detal re-uploadda bayroqni jim yo'qotadi.

**Tekshiruv:** snapshot_custom_routes hali INNER join + `PartRoute.source == RouteSource.MANUAL` (82) bilan yig'adi; matrix.py:116-129 is_custom yoqilganda MANUAL qator yaratmaydi (faqat AUTO soft-delete/restore). parents lug'ati (61-71) faqat saved.setdefault orqali ishlatiladi, restore esa saved.items() bo'ylab yuradi — MANUAL qatorsiz nostandart/biriktirilgan detal re-uploadda is_custom va assembly_parent_id ni jimgina yo'qotadi.

**Tuzatish:** Snapshot the flags from `part` itself (LEFT OUTER JOIN to PartRoute, or a second query over `Part.is_custom == True` / `Part.assembly_parent_id IS NOT NULL`) and merge the manual process codes into that dict, so is_custom + assembly_parent_id survive even with an empty code list.

### `backend/app/services/giblab/parser.py:463` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

CS `<part>` ro'yxatidagi HAR bir stubga `label_num` beriladi, joylashtirilmagan (usedCount=0) detalga ham — ingest esa aynan `label_num is not None` ni "listga joylashtirilgan" belgisi deb oladi va soxta ARRA qadamini yaratadi.

**Dalil:** ref_part.label_num = label_counter

**Repro:** `uv run python r20.py`: _parse_cs_operation ga 2 stub (usedCount=1 va usedCount=0) berildi → p1 label_num=1, p2 (usedCount=0) label_num=2; ingest.py:387 sharti `project.generation != "processed" or pp.material_name or pp.label_num is not None` ikkalasiga ham True → joylashtirilmagan detalga ARRA qadami. Funksiya stubning usedCount atributini umuman o'qimaydi (kod: for stub in op_el.findall("part") → faqat id). docs/10:402 ham usedCount=0 = ataylab joylashtirilmagan deb belgilaydi.

**Tekshiruv:** _parse_cs_operation hali stubning usedCount ini o'qimaydi — har stubga label_num/material_name beriladi; ingest.py:391 `if pp.material_name or pp.label_num is not None:` shu belgiga qarab ARRA qadamini yaratadi, ya'ni usedCount=0 detalga soxta ARRA qadami ochiladi. ingest.py:475 dagi ghosts ogohlantirishi faqat xabar beradi, qadamni olib tashlamaydi.

**Tuzatish:** `_parse_cs_operation` da stubning `usedCount` ini o'qib, `_f(stub, "usedCount") == 0` bo'lsa `label_num`/`material_name` ni BERMASLIK (label_counter esa GibSaw tartibi uchun baribir oshirilsin), yoki `ParsedPart` ga alohida `placed: bool` maydon qo'shib ingest'dagi `sheet_part` shartini o'shanga bog'lash.

### `backend/app/services/labels.py:230` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

Loyiha nomi 26 belgigacha chiziladi, lekin QR bloki uni x=40 mm dan boshlab ustidan bosib o'chiradi — uzun loyiha nomining oxiri chekda umuman ko'rinmaydi.

**Dalil:** text(5, 13.5, d.project_name[:26], 3.3, bold=True)

**Repro:** `uv run python r21.py`: LiberationSans-Bold 3.3mm da 26 belgili 'Karimov Sherzod oshxona 26' textlength=45.5mm → x 5..50.5mm; textbbox px (40,113,404,132), QR paste box (320,8,448,136) — kesishadi x 320..404, y 113..132. render_label_png haqiqiy PNG'da faqat 19 belgi ('Karimov Sherzod osh') ko'rinadi, 'xona 26' QR ostida qoladi (rasm: scratchpad/p2/label21.png). Hozirgi satr labels.py:204 (208 emas), matn o'zgarmagan.

### `backend/app/services/scanning.py:518` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

QR text is not order-scoped (uniqueness is only per order), so when two live PRODUCTION orders are built from the same GibLab product/part codes, every scan is silently credited to the NEWEST order — and the printed label carries no order number to tell them apart.

**Dalil:** .order_by(Order.id.desc())

**Repro:** `uv run python r22.py r22b.py`: (1) ParsedPart.qr_texts → ['4202_8_1','4202_8_2'] — buyurtma raqami yo'q; (2) PartInstance yagona indeks ix_part_instance_order_qr=['order_id','qr_text'] — bir qr_text ikki buyurtmada ruxsat etilgan; (3) record_scan_at ni FakeDB bilan chaqirib 2-statement kompilyatsiya qilindi: `... WHERE part_instance.qr_text='4202_8' AND ... "order".status IN ('production','cancelled') ORDER BY "order".id DESC` — LIMIT/count yo'q, iste'molchi .first(); ko'plik tekshiruvi yo'q. LabelData (labels.py:20-40) da order_number maydoni yo'q, u faqat pachka(:431)/waste(:517) dataclass'larida.

**Tekshiruv:** giblab/models.py:128 `return f"{self.product_code}_{self.code}"` — qr_text buyurtmaga bog'lanmagan, indeks (order_id, qr_text) (order.py:133); scanning.py:511-521 hali ham jimgina eng yangi jonli buyurtmani oladi, kollizyani sezmaydi. Detal yorlig'i render'ida order_number chizilmaydi (faqat :484 pachka va :551 waste).

**Tuzatish:** Make the collision impossible or visible: (a) prefix qr_text with the order (e.g. `{order.number}_{key}`) at ingest and regenerate SCX/labels, or (b) at minimum detect it — count matching rows in record_scan_at and raise `ScanRejected("E001", "Bu QR bir nechta buyurtmada bor — ...")` when more than one live order matches, instead of silently taking `Order.id.desc()`. Also draw `LabelData.order_number` on the label (it is already loaded at labels.py:104 and never rendered) so the operator can see which order a chek belongs to.

### `backend/app/services/scanning.py:656` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

The `except IntegrityError` guard around `db.flush()` never fires — SQLAlchemy autoflushes the pending Scan INSERT inside the preceding `db.execute(update(...))`, so a `client_event_id` unique-index race escapes as an unhandled 500 instead of the intended 422 E001.

**Dalil:** await db.execute(update(PartRoute).where(PartRoute.id == route.id).values(total_done=PartRoute.total_done + 1))

**Repro:** `uv run python r23.py` (sqlalchemy 2.0.52, app local_session autoflush=True tasdiqlandi): sqlite'da unique cid bilan Scan avval commit qilingan; yangi sessiyada db.add(Scan(cid=dup)) → db.execute(update(Route)...) → IntegrityError shu execute() dan chiqdi ('raised as a result of Query-invoked autoflush'), flush() gacha yetmadi. Ya'ni scanning.py:651 dagi execute(update(PartRoute)) try tashqarisida IntegrityError beradi, :653-657 guard o'lik.

**Tekshiruv:** Tuzilma o'sha-o'sha: db.add(scan) (:652) → execute(update(PartRoute...)) (:656) → try: flush (:658-662). core/db.py:26 sessiyada autoflush=False yo'q, demak Scan INSERT execute() ichida autoflush bo'lib, client_event_id unique poygasi try tashqarisida 500 beradi; guard o'lik. db2382a scanning.py ni o'zgartirgan, lekin bu blokka tegmagan.

**Tuzatish:** Move the `db.add(scan)` + flush before the counter UPDATE, or wrap the whole block (lines 587-594) in the `try`: `try: db.add(scan); await db.flush(); await db.execute(update(PartRoute)...); await db.refresh(route, ["total_done"]) except IntegrityError: ...`. Alternatively call `db.execute(..., execution_options={"autoflush": False})` — but the flush must then still be inside the try.

## frontend

### `frontend/src/components/Orders/CustomRoutes.tsx:124-125` — BUG · yangi sweep · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

"Bekor qilish" (Cancel) tugmasi faqat setDraft({}) chaqiradi, setParents({}) emas. dirty holati draft VA parents ikkalasidan hisoblanadi (Object.keys(draft).length + Object.keys(parents).length > 0), lekin cancel faqat draft'ni tozalaydi. mutation.onSuccess (68-72 qatorlar) esa ikkalasini ham to'g'ri tozalaydi — demak muallif ikkalasini ham reset qilish kerakligini bilgan, faqat cancel tugmasida unutgan.

**Dalil:** <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => setDraft({})}>

**Repro:** node scratchpad/r37.mjs — komponent holat mantiqini so'zma-so'z simulyatsiya: parent select o'zgartirilgach parents={12:7}, dirty=true; 'Bekor qilish' (setDraft({})) dan keyin dirty=true, parents={"12":7} qoladi; keyingi Saqlash ids=['12'] bilan assembly_parent_id:7 yuboradi. grep: setParents({}) faqat onSuccess (71-qator) da chaqiriladi, cancel'da yo'q.

**Tekshiruv:** Cancel tugmasi hali faqat setDraft({}) chaqiradi, setParents({}) yo'q (onSuccess 70-71 esa ikkalasini tozalaydi) — regressiya tuzatilmagan.

**Tuzatish:** onClick={() => { setDraft({}); setParents({}) }} qilib ikkalasini ham tozalash kerak.

### `frontend/src/components/Orders/CustomRoutes.tsx:88` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

codesOf() reads every saved route step, including the system tail steps (15 UPAKOVKA / 16 TMO) that the backend appends itself, so a re-edited custom route is saved back with the tail embedded in the middle and a second tail appended.

**Repro:** `node r24.mjs`: codesOf/toggle mantiqi izolyatsiyada — saqlangan routes [1,3,15,16] → codesOf=[1,3,15,16], mine=16 (TMO); toggle(6) → route_codes=[1,3,15,16,6]. `uv run python`: PROCESS_POSTS da 15,16 bor → _set_custom_route validatsiyasi o'tadi, natijaviy PartRoute kodlari [1,3,15,16,6,15,16] (dum o'rtada + ikkinchi dum). Backend read_order_matrix (matrix.py:38-51) hamma PartRoute'ni filtrsiz qaytaradi; frontendda TAIL filtri yo'q (grep TAIL frontend/src → 0).

**Tekshiruv:** Kod oqimi to'liq kuzatildi: codesOf hali ham dum (15/16) filtrsiz; backend read_order_matrix hamma PartRoute ni qaytaradi (matrix.py:49-51, filtrsiz), _set_custom_route esa dumni har doim qo'shadi (:342-344) va 15/16 PROCESS_POSTS da bor, ya'ni validatsiya ularni o'tkazadi. Saqlangan marshrutni qayta tahrirlash toggle orqali dumli ro'yxatga qadam qo'shadi → dum o'rtada qoladi + ikkinchi dum qo'shiladi. mine=codes[length-1] ham har saqlangan detalda 16 bo'lib, biriktirish filtri buziladi. Haqiqiy bug, tuzatish kerak (TAIL_CODES filtri — bir qatorlik).

**Tuzatish:** Filter the tail out when reading: `routes.map(r => r.process_code).filter(c => !TAIL_CODES.includes(c))` with `TAIL_CODES = [15, 16]` added to `@/lib/mes` (backend `giblab/models.py:52` has the same tuple). This also fixes `mine = codes[codes.length - 1]` (line 209), which currently resolves to 16/TMO for every saved part and drives both the assembly-parent option filter and the "Marshrut … da tugaydi" hint.

### `frontend/src/components/Orders/OrderMatrix.tsx:248` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

Ticking "Nostandart" only locks FUTURE stage clicks; stage toggles already staged for that part are still sent in the same batch, which is exactly the collision the MatrixCell comment claims is prevented.

### `frontend/src/components/Terminal/useTerminalScan.ts:68` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

Frontend da'vosi (ikki POST) to'liq isbotlangan, oqibat da'vodan torroq.

**Tekshiruv:** Beyjik shoxi hali har qanday cooled() tekshiruvidan oldin turadi va terminal.tsx:344 onBadge to'g'ridan-to'g'ri startShift.mutate chaqiradi (isPending guard yo'q) — skaner double-read ikki POST /terminal/session poygasini beradi (ikkitasi ham eski smenani yopib yangisini ochadi). ### `frontend/src/hooks/useMesWs.ts:25` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

The 30s heartbeat is write-only: the server's `pong` is explicitly discarded and no timer ever checks that a reply arrived, so a half-open WebSocket leaves `online === true` forever and every live event is silently lost.

**Repro:** repro/d_ws.tsx (real useMesWs, globalThis.WebSocket = yarim-ochiq fake: onopen keladi, send() jim yutadi, hech qachon pong/close bermaydi; setInterval/setTimeout 1000x siqilgan): online=true bo'ldi; pong JSON handler'ga bormadi (kutilgan); 10 heartbeat davri pong'siz -> ping yuborildi=11, close()=0, reconnect=0, online=true (o'zgarmadi). Hook ichida lastPong/liveness tekshiruvi yo'q (grep 'pong' faqat 33-qatorda, tashlab yuborish uchun). Eslatma (banddagi note to'g'ri): real brauzerda o'lik TCP'ga yozish oxir-oqibat onclose beradi, ya'ni 'forever' mubolag'a; lekin hookning o'zida liveness mexanizmi yo'qligi bajarib isbotlandi. docs/05:301 ham pong'ni 'filtrlanadi' deb yozadi — hujjat kod bilan mos, va'da liveness emas.

**Tekshiruv:** Heartbeat hali write-only: pong tashlab yuboriladi, lastPong tekshiruvi yo'q, online'ni faqat onclose o'chiradi. Half-open ulanishda live eventlar yo'qoladi va indikator yolg'on gapiradi. Eslatma: o'lik soketga 30s ping yozish TCP orqali oxir-oqibat (daqiqalar ichida) onclose'ni chaqiradi, ya'ni "forever" mubolag'a — lekin docs/05:279 va'da qilgan liveness mexanizmi baribir yo'q, fix arzon.

**Tuzatish:** Stamp `lastPongAt` in `onmessage` when `event.type === "pong"`, and in the same 30s interval close the socket (forcing the existing reconnect path) when no pong has arrived for ~2 intervals. Only then does `online` mean what docs/05:279, the dashboard pill and the terminal "ALOQA YO'Q" block claim it means.

### `frontend/src/hooks/useScanFlash.ts:43` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

useScanFlash's auto-scroll timer is always cancelled before it fires, because every call site passes inline getKey/getCount lambdas that change identity on the re-render caused by setFlashId.

**Repro:** repro/e_flash.tsx (real useScanFlash, jsdom, Element.prototype.scrollIntoView spy; parts [{1,0},{2,0}] -> [{1,0},{2,1}] rerender, 400 ms kutish): inline lambda (KromkaView.tsx:90-95 / PrisadkaView.tsx:114-130 kabi) -> flashId=2, scrollIntoView chaqiruvlari=0. Xuddi shu ssenariy useCallback bilan barqaror getKey/getCount -> flashId=2, scrollIntoView=1 ['kv-row-2']. Ya'ni setFlashId re-render'ida dep massividagi yangi lambda identity cleanup'ni ishga tushirib 150ms timerni bekor qiladi; PrisadkaView.tsx:122 instRows ham memoizatsiyasiz. Flash (chap chiziq) ishlaydi, avto-scroll (docs/05:295 va'dasi) hech qachon ishlamaydi.

**Tekshiruv:** Kod aynan avvalgidek: setFlashId re-render'ida call site'lardagi inline getKey/getCount yangi identity oladi -> cleanup 150ms timerni bekor qiladi -> re-run'da prevRef yangilangani uchun changed=null, scroll bo'lmaydi. KromkaView:90 va PrisadkaView:114/125 hali inline lambda, instRows memoizatsiyasiz. Xulq iqtibos-tasdiqlangan React semantikasi, lekin bajarib isbotlanmagan (repro yozilmadi).

**Tuzatish:** Keep the scroll out of the dep-sensitive path: store `changed` in a ref and do the scroll from a separate effect keyed on `flashId` only (`useEffect(() => {...}, [flashId, rowIdPrefix])`), or drop getKey/getCount from the dep array (they are read through a ref like `handler.current` in useMesWs/useBadgeScan). Also memoize `instRows` in PrisadkaView so the hook body does not rebuild its Map on every render.

### `frontend/src/routes/_layout/employees.tsx:389-399` — BUG · yangi sweep · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

TogglePerson (Nofaol qilish/Faollashtirish) uses `if (p.employee) {...} else if (p.user) {...}` — mutually exclusive branches. For a person with BOTH an employee record and a linked user account (access="both", the badge+password case the file's own header comment calls out as a first-class state), only `employee.status` gets flipped; `user.is_active` is never touched. Login (`backend/app/api/routes/login.py:100`, `deps.py:38`) checks `user.is_active` directly and has no dependency on employee.status, so the person's password login keeps working after being "deactivated". This contradicts both the component's own doc comment ("Hodimda `status`, hisobda `is_active` — ikkalasi bir tugmadan boshqariladi") and the confirm-dialog text shown to the admin ("beyjigi va kirishi ishlamaydi" — implies login is also cut off). The sibling DeletePerson mutationFn in the same file handles this correctly with two independent `if` statements (not if/else-if), confirming the else-if here is the anomaly, not the intended design.

**Dalil:** } else if (p.user) {

**Repro:** node scratchpad/r38.mjs — mutationFn if/else-if mantiqi stub servislar bilan: person{employee:{status:active}, user:{is_active:true}} (access='both'), 'Nofaol qilish' -> chaqiruvlar: [updateEmployee {status:inactive}] xolos, updateUser chaqirilmadi. Backend: employees.py update_employee da is_active umuman yo'q (grep bo'sh), SQLAlchemy event/listens_for yo'q (grep bo'sh); login.py:100 va deps.py:38 faqat user.is_active tekshiradi — parol bilan kirish davom etadi, dialog matni 'kirishi ishlamaydi' va doc-komment esa aksini aytadi. Tugma p.user bo'lsa canManageAccounts talab qiladi (308-qator), demak updateUser huquqi bor. `else` ni olib tashlash (DeletePerson naqshi) — arzon va xavfsiz.

**Tekshiruv:** TogglePerson mutationFn hali if/else-if: employee va user mavjud bo'lsa faqat employee.status yangilanadi, user.is_active tegilmaydi — access='both' odam nofaol qilingach ham parol bilan kiraveradi.

**Tuzatish:** In TogglePerson's mutationFn, update employee and user independently (mirror DeletePerson's pattern): `if (p.employee) { await EmployeesService.updateEmployee(...) }` followed by a separate `if (p.user) { await UsersService.updateUser({ path: { user_id: Number(p.user.id) }, body: { is_active: !active } }) }` (drop the `else`), so a person with both a badge and a password login actually loses both when deactivated.

"Jami: {total}" (205-qator) ham 0 ga tushadi.

### `frontend/src/routes/login.tsx:136` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

Post-tanlash dialogida ikkinchi beyjik so'rovi xato bersa, xato HECH QAYERDA ko'rsatilmaydi — dialog qotib qoladi.

**Dalil:** badge.isError && !choice ? extractErrorMessage(badge.error) : ""

**Repro:** `node -e` bilan login.tsx:136-137 ifodasini so'zma-so'z ishga tushirdim: choice=null & isError -> "Server xatosi 500"; choice o'rnatilgan & isError -> "" (bo'sh). `open={!!choice}` -> true (dialog ochiq qoladi). `sed -n 299,332p | grep badgeError|alert|destructive` -> rc=1 (dialog ichida xato elementi YO'Q). `grep -n badgeError` -> faqat 135/192/197, ya'ni dialogdan TASHQARIDA chiziladi.

**Tekshiruv:** onError (90-103) hali faqat POST_CHOICE ni ushlaydi; dialogdagi post tugmasi (321-323) badge.mutate chaqiradi, 400/500 kelsa choice tozalanmaydi, badgeError !choice sharti bilan bostiriladi, dialog ichida alert bloki yo'q. Qo'lda yopish esa badge.reset() qiladi (308) — xato hech qachon ko'rinmaydi. Kichik fix.

**Tuzatish:** `badgeError` shartidan `!choice` ni olib tashlash (yoki dialog ichida alohida `role="alert"` blok chizish) va `onError` da POST_CHOICE bo'lmagan xatoda `setChoice(null)` qilish — hozir 400/500 javob operatorga umuman ko'rinmaydi.

**Tuzatish:** Add `onError: (err) => { toast.error(detailMessage(err)); setEndFor(null) }` to the `endSession` mutation, matching the other three mutations in the file.

### `frontend/src/routes/terminal.tsx:387` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

The undo ConfirmDialog turns into a dead modal: the 15s auto-clear nulls `lastScan` while the dialog is open, after which the confirm button is a silent no-op and the dialog never closes by itself.

**Dalil:** const t = setTimeout(() => setLastScan(null), 15_000)

**Repro:** O'sha harness: lastScan=ok, setUndoOpen(true); advance(15000) → lastScan=null, undoOpen=true (dialog ochiq qoladi, ConfirmDialog.tsx o'zi yopmaydi); onConfirm() bosildi → undoMutate chaqiruvlari [] (jim no-op), undoOpen hali true. Tuzatish: 'hech qachon yopilmaydi' qismi yumshagan — onOpenChange={setUndoOpen} (761) bor, operator 'Yopish'/Esc bilan qo'lda yopa oladi; lekin tasdiqlash tugmasi 15s dan keyin jim no-op — bu proven.

**Tekshiruv:** 15s auto-clear (385-389) undoOpen holatini tekshirmaydi; onConfirm (751-753) hanuz lastScan?.kind === 'ok' && undoMutation.mutate(lastScan.scanId) — 15s dan keyin tasdiqlash jim no-op, description bo'sh bo'ladi. onOpenChange={setUndoOpen} (740) qo'lda yopishga imkon beradi, lekin jim no-op qoldi. scanId ni ochilishda snapshot qilish yoki muddat o'tganda dialogni yopish — kichik fix.

**Tuzatish:** Close the dialog when the scan expires (e.g. add `if (undoOpen) return` to the 15s effect, or `useEffect(() => { if (!lastScan) setUndoOpen(false) }, [lastScan])`), and snapshot `scanId` into state when the dialog opens instead of reading `lastScan` at confirm time.

## infra

### `docker-compose.prod.yml:55` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`${TAG:-latest}` default hech qachon mavjud emas — CI faqat `:<git sha>` tegini push qiladi, ya'ni hujjatda ko'rsatilgan `make docker-prod` (TAG'siz) `manifest unknown` bilan yiqiladi.

**Repro:** python yaml bilan .github/workflows/ci.yml parse: barcha `tags:` = faqat `ghcr.io/<owner>/mebelix-mes-<name>:${{ github.sha }}`; image teglarida 'latest' grep → 0 natija. Makefile docker-prod TAG'siz `compose pull` qiladi → ${TAG:-latest} → :latest; docs/07:95 'so'ng make docker-prod' deb yozadi. Registry tomonini bevosita tasdiqlab bo'lmadi: `docker manifest inspect ...:latest` → unauthorized, `gh api packages` → 403 (read:packages scope yo'q). Lekin CI yagona push manbai va u :latest yaratmaydi — determinlashgan.

**Tekshiruv:** Holat o'zgarmagan: CI hanuz faqat :sha teg push qiladi (ci.yml:121), :latest hech qayerda yaratilmaydi — hujjatlangan TAG'siz 'make docker-prod' (docs/07:95) manifest unknown bilan yiqiladi. Fix arzon (CI'ga latest teg yoki ${TAG:?} + docs yangilash); prod deploy deploy.sh orqali TAG bilan ishlagani uchun shoshilinch emas, lekin qo'llanma yo'li buzuq.

**Tuzatish:** Yo CI'da `:latest` tegini ham push qilish (`tags:` ga ikkinchi qator), yo `${TAG:?TAG majburiy}` qilib defaultni olib tashlash va docs/07:84,95 dagi "defaultlar lokal qo'lda ishga tushirishga" jumlasini `make docker-prod TAG=<sha>` ko'rinishiga yangilash.

### `scripts/backup.sh:27` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`env_get` strips everything from the first `#` onward, so any `#` inside POSTGRES_USER / POSTGRES_DB / ADMIN_PASSWORD / TG_BOT_TOKEN silently truncates the value — the DB dump gets encrypted with a password that is not the admin password, making every backup undecryptable by the documented recipe.

**Dalil:** sed -n "s/^${1}=//p" "$2" | tail -1 | sed 's/[[:space:]]*#.*$//; s/^"\(.*\)"$/\1/; s/^'"'"'\(.*\)'"'"'$/\1/'

**Repro:** env_get ni t.env ustida bajarildi: ADMIN_PASSWORD=Secret#2026 → [Secret]; POSTGRES_USER="app#1" → ["app] (qo'shtirnoq ham buziladi, chunki izoh-kesish unquote'dan oldin); TG_BOT_TOKEN=123:abc#xyz → [123:abc]. Taqqos: backend uv run python dotenv_values → 'Secret#2026', 'app#1', '123:abc#xyz' (pydantic to'liq qiymatni o'qiydi). Roundtrip: env_get bergan parol bilan openssl enc, haqiqiy ADMIN_PASSWORD bilan dec → 'bad decrypt'. :? guard bo'sh bo'lmagan kesik qiymatni o'tkazib yuboradi — jim, ochib bo'lmaydigan backup.

**Tekshiruv:** env_get o'zgarmagan; maqsad daraxtdagi helperni probe .env bilan ISHGA TUSHIRDIM: ADMIN_PASSWORD="Str0ng#Pass!2026" -> ["Str0ng], TG_BOT_TOKEN=123:AAF#xyz -> [123:AAF]. Parol jim kesiladi, backup (79: -pass env:ADMIN_PASSWORD) noto'g'ri parol bilan shifrlanadi, :? guard ushlamaydi — tiklab bo'lmas data-loss.

**Tuzatish:** Only strip a comment that is preceded by whitespace and is outside quotes, or drop comment-stripping entirely and strip quotes only. Minimal fix: `sed 's/[[:space:]]\{1,\}#.*$//; s/^"\(.*\)"$/\1/; ...'` — and additionally strip quotes *before* comment handling so `"a#b"` survives. Safest is to read values with `grep -m1` + a quote-strip only, dropping the `#` rule.  ---

### `backend/Dockerfile:16` — BUG · yangi sweep · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

Builder stage installs the full 'dev' dependency group (mypy, ruff, pre-commit, types-openpyxl, plus their transitive deps like nodeenv/virtualenv/identify) into the production image because --no-dev is missing; confirmed by running the exact command with UV_PROJECT_ENVIRONMENT pointed at a scratch dir — it installs mypy==2.3.1, ruff==0.16.3, pre-commit==4.6.2, types-openpyxl, virtualenv, nodeenv, identify, cfgv, distlib alongside the runtime deps.

**Dalil:** uv sync --frozen --no-install-project

**Repro:** cd backend && UV_PROJECT_ENVIRONMENT=<scratch> uv sync --frozen --no-install-project --dry-run -> 'Would install 56 packages' (mypy==2.3.1, ruff==0.16.5, pre-commit==4.6.2, types-openpyxl, virtualenv kiradi); --no-dev bilan -> 39 paket, dev-paketlar yo'q. pyproject.toml:36-42 [dependency-groups].dev.

**Tekshiruv:** Hali ham --no-dev yo'q, builder bosqichida dev-guruh (mypy, ruff, pre-commit va h.k.) prod image'ga o'rnatilishda qoladi.

**Tuzatish:** Add --no-dev (or --group prod-only equivalent) to the uv sync call so the runtime image only gets the [project] dependencies, e.g. `uv sync --frozen --no-install-project --no-dev`.

download_order_scx_zip zips only SCX files that happen to exist on disk and silently drops any OrderFile row whose file is missing — no error, no warning in the response. **Dalil:** if full_path.is_file():

**Tekshiruv:** download_order_scx_zip hali yo'q faylni jim tashlab ketadi (149-150), download_order_file esa xuddi shu holat uchun 404 beradi (276-277) — nomuvofiqlik hali bor.

**Tuzatish:** Track rows whose full_path.is_file() is False and either raise/append a warning (e.g. include missing filenames in a response header or reject with 404/409 listing them) instead of silently omitting them from the ZIP, matching the failure behavior of download_order_file.

### `backend/app/api/routes/orders/files.py:65,85` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

The whole .project ingest — XML parse, blob decode, cut-map build, SCX XML generation and one synchronous write per SCX file — runs inline on the event loop, freezing every terminal, TABLO and WebSocket in the worker for the duration of the upload.

**Dalil:** project = parse_project(content)

**Repro:** inspect: 'run_in_threadpool' absent from files.py and ingest.py; parse_project is a plain sync function (ET.fromstring). Synthetic 1.78 MB project (1500 products, 3000 XNC parts) built from uploads/projects/2/x.project: parse_project = 573 ms, build_scx_files for all parts = 2582 ms — all executed inline in the coroutine, plus 3000 sync write_text calls in write_and_register_scx (ingest.py:251). MAX_PROJECT_FILE_SIZE is 50 MB (config.py:74, corpus '< 5 MB').

**Tekshiruv:** Upload path moved to orders/files.py but still runs the whole ingest inline on the event loop -- parse_project(content) up to 50MB at :65, ingest_project at :85 with per-file write_text loops still in ingest.py; grep for run_in_threadpool in files.py returns nothing. Blocks every terminal/TABLO/WS in the worker during upload; precedent (security.py's run_in_threadpool) exists.

**Tuzatish:** Wrap the CPU/disk phases in `run_in_threadpool`: `project = await run_in_threadpool(parse_project, content)`, and move the file-writing part of `write_and_register_scx` (build the (filename, content) list first, then `await run_in_threadpool(_write_all, ...)`). The same treatment is needed for `excel.xlsx_response` (`wb.save(buf)` at excel.py:41), `orders.download_order_scx_zip` (`zipfile.ZipFile(...).write` loop) and `terminal.render_instance_label` (`render_label_png` at terminal.py:1120).

### `backend/app/api/routes/orders/files.py:82-89` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

wipe_previous_parse unlinks the old .project/SCX files from disk inside an uncommitted transaction; if ingest/restore later raises ParseError the DB is rolled back but the files are already gone.

**Dalil:** await wipe_previous_parse(db, order, root)

**Repro:** uv run python with a temp dir + AsyncMock db reproducing files.py:83-89 verbatim: two real files on disk -> `await wipe_previous_parse(db, order, root)` -> "after wipe (BEFORE any commit): False False" (unlink is at ingest.py:224, inside the function, before any commit) -> raise ParseError -> `await db.rollback()` (1 call, db.commit await_count == 0) -> "after rollback: project exists = False | scx exists = False". The DB UPDATE is_deleted=True is rolled back, so the OrderFile rows come back alive pointing at paths that no longer exist.

**Tekshiruv:** wipe_previous_parse (ingest.py:205-225) hamon commit'dan OLDIN eski .project/SCX fayllarni diskdan o'chiradi; files.py:83-89 da keyingi ingest_project/restore_custom_routes ParseError bersa db.rollback() DB qatorlarini tiriltiradi, lekin fayllar allaqachon yo'q.

**Tuzatish:** Collect the paths to delete and perform the unlink only after the caller commits (return them from wipe_previous_parse and delete in upload_order_project after `await db.commit()`); likewise write the new .project/SCX bytes to disk only after the commit, or delete them in the except branch.

### `backend/app/api/routes/orders/lifecycle.py:91,97-106,121` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

On the number-collision retry path create_order reads client.full_name after db.rollback() has expired every identity-map object, so the documented retry ends in a lazy-load error instead of a 201.

**Dalil:** client = await crud_clients.get(db, id=body.client_id)     if client is None:         raise NotFoundException("Mijoz topilmadi.")

**Repro:** uv run --with aiosqlite python scratchpad/repro_rollback_expire.py — app bilan bir xil async_sessionmaker(expire_on_commit=False), create_order sikli aynan takrorlandi: attempt 0 IntegrityError -> rollback; attempt 1 da `current_user.id` (:86) -> MissingGreenlet (deps.get_current_user SHU SessionDep dan yuklaydi); uid ni qotirib o'tilganda retry oxirida `client.full_name` -> MissingGreenlet. Ya'ni retry hujjatlangan 201 o'rniga 500 beradi, da'vo qilinganidan ham ertaroq yiqiladi.

**Tekshiruv:** client is still loaded once before the 3-attempt retry loop; db.rollback() on IntegrityError (:104) still runs, and db.py's async_sessionmaker uses expire_on_commit=False only (default expire_on_rollback=True), so a successful retry after one rollback still reads client.full_name (:121) on an expired ORM object outside an awaited DB call -- MissingGreenlet risk on the documented retry path. Quote-verified, not executed, but the session config confirms the mechanism.

**Tuzatish:** Capture the name before the loop (`client_name = client.full_name`) and return `_order_read(order, client_name)`, so no ORM attribute is read after a rollback.

### `backend/app/api/routes/orders/matrix.py:134-147` — BUG · yangi sweep · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

_check_assembly_match (which enforces that a child part's last route step exists in its assembly parent's route) only runs when assembly_parent_id itself changes in this edit. If a part is already attached to a parent (from an earlier request) and the technolog later edits only that part's route_codes (assembly_parent_id untouched, so parent_changed is False), the new route's last step is never re-validated against the parent's route, so the child can end up with a last step that the parent never scans — exactly the stuck-assembly failure the check's own docstring says it prevents ('Mos kelmasa birikish hech qachon ro'y bermasdi').

**Dalil:** if parent_changed and part.assembly_parent_id is not None:             await _check_assembly_match(db, part)

**Repro:** edit_order_matrix'ni fake db + patch(_get_order,_require_editable,_set_custom_route,_check_assembly_match,log_action,read_order_matrix) bilan chaqirdim; part.assembly_parent_id=42, body=MatrixPartEdit(part_id=1, route_codes=[7,11]) → 'assembly_parent_id' in model_fields_set=False, _set_custom_route await_count=1, _check_assembly_match await_count=0. grep: _check_assembly_match'ning yagona chaqiruvi 143-qatorda `parent_changed` sharti ostida; lifecycle start/scanning'da qayta tekshiruv yo'q (scanning.py:395 mos kelmasa `continue`).

**Tekshiruv:** _check_assembly_match hali faqat parent_changed=True bo'lganda chaqiriladi; route_codes o'zgarganda (parent o'zgarmasa) chaqirilmaydi — asl bug o'zgarmagan.

**Tuzatish:** Also call _check_assembly_match when route_codes changes for a part that already has assembly_parent_id set (i.e. run the check whenever the child's own route was rebuilt, not only when parent_changed).

### `backend/app/commands/seed_defaults.py:138` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

Post-account phone numbers are derived from the POSITION of the role inside POST_ROLES, so inserting a tenth post role anywhere but at the end makes the seed try to insert a phone an existing account already owns — a UNIQUE violation that aborts prestart and blocks the whole deploy.

**Dalil:** phone=f"+9980000000{index:02d}",

**Repro:** uv run python r1_seed_phone.py: fake AsyncSession, get_password_hash mocked. Fresh run assigns arrachi..omborchi -> +998000000001..09. Then POST_ROLES patched with a new role at position 3 and the 9 existing accounts fed back: seed tries INSERT ('admin', '+998000000003') -> COLLISION, phone owned by 'ovalchi' (partial UNIQUE ix_user_phone). prestart.sh (set -e) runs seed_defaults on every container start; git shows OVALCHI was inserted mid-list on 2026-08-01, account seeding came 2026-08-19 — so it has not bitten yet only by ordering luck.

**Tekshiruv:** Telefon hamon enumerate(POST_ROLES.items(), start=1) indeksidan olinadi (127-q.) — POST_ROLES o'rtasiga yangi rol qo'shilsa mavjud hisoblarning telefoni bilan to'qnashib UNIQUE violation prestart'ni yiqitadi. Kod o'zgarmagan.

**Tuzatish:** Derive the phone from something stable instead of the loop index — e.g. a literal `"phone"` key inside each POST_ROLES entry, or `f"+998000000{list(UserRole).index(role):03d}"` pinned once — or make the insert tolerant: look up by `username=str(role)` first (already unique per role) and skip/patch instead of inserting a positional phone.

### `backend/app/commands/seed_defaults.py:159` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

PATCH /posts/{id} lets an admin change order_index, but seed_defaults resets it to the hardcoded table on every container start, so the edit is silently reverted at the next deploy.

**Dalil:** elif post.order_index != data["order_index"]:

**Repro:** `seed_defaults()` soxta sessiya bilan (local_session va _seed_post_accounts monkeypatch qilindi), mavjud ARRA posti `order_index=9` (admin PATCH qilgan holat): BEFORE seed = 9 → AFTER seed = 1, log: `Post ARRA order_index -> 1`. PATCH tomoni: posts.py:25-26 `for field, value in body.model_dump(exclude_unset=True).items(): setattr(post, field, value)` — schemas/posts.py:23 `order_index: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1)` ochiq. Ishga tushish: backend/scripts/prestart.sh → `python -m app.commands.seed_defaults`, docker-compose.prod.yml:62 command'ida har start'da chaqiriladi.

**Tekshiruv:** elif shoxi hamon har deploy'da post.order_index'ni jadvaldagi qiymatga qaytaradi, schemas/posts.py:23 esa PATCH orqali order_index tahrirlashga ruxsat beradi (o'zgarmagan) — admin tahriri keyingi konteyner ishga tushishida jimgina yo'qoladi.

**Tuzatish:** Pick one owner. Either drop `order_index` from `PostUpdate` (schemas/posts.py:23) so the seed is authoritative, or restrict the seed's sync to newly-inserted posts (delete the `elif` branch at seed_defaults.py:162-166).

### `backend/app/core/config.py:126-128` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

The Alembic DB URL is built by raw f-string interpolation, so a POSTGRES_PASSWORD (or user/db) containing `@`, `/`, `:`, `#` or `?` silently produces a wrong host/user/database instead of failing loudly.

**Dalil:** credentials = f"{self.POSTGRES_USER}:{self.POSTGRES_PASSWORD}"

**Repro:** `POSTGRES_PASSWORD='p@ss/w:rd'` bilan `Settings().POSTGRES_URI` = `mes:p@ss/w:rd@db:5432/mes`; `make_url('postgresql+asyncpg://'+uri)` → **host='ss', user='mes', db='w:rd@db:5432/mes', pwd='p'**. `quote_plus` bilan qurilganda → host='db', db='mes', pwd='p@ss/w:rd'. Iste'molchilar: db.py:14 `DATABASE_URL = POSTGRES_ASYNC_PREFIX + POSTGRES_URI`, migrations/env.py:16 `config.set_main_option("sqlalchemy.url", DATABASE_URL)`.

**Tekshiruv:** The two-places duplication (item 123) is fixed, but the underlying encoding bug survives in the single remaining place: POSTGRES_URI still builds credentials/location via raw f-string, no quote_plus or URL.create. A password containing @, /, :, # or ? still breaks the DSN, and env.py now inherits it via DATABASE_URL.

**Tuzatish:** Percent-encode the credentials before interpolation, e.g. `from urllib.parse import quote_plus` and use `quote_plus(settings.POSTGRES_USER)` / `quote_plus(settings.POSTGRES_PASSWORD)`, or build the URL with `sqlalchemy.engine.URL.create(...)`. (`backend/app/core/config.py:116-119` `POSTGRES_URI` has the identical defect and should be fixed the same way.)

### `backend/app/core/config.py:127` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`POSTGRES_URI` interpolates the raw `POSTGRES_PASSWORD` into a DSN without percent-encoding, so any password containing a URL-reserved character produces a malformed connection URL.

**Dalil:** credentials = f"{self.POSTGRES_USER}:{self.POSTGRES_PASSWORD}"

**Repro:** uv run python r3_dsn.py with POSTGRES_PASSWORD='p@ss/w:rd#1': DSN 'postgresql+asyncpg://postgres:p@ss/w:rd#1@localhost:5432/mebelix'; sqlalchemy make_url parses it as password='p' host='ss' db='w:rd#1@localhost:5432/mebelix'. Same string with quote_plus parses correctly (password intact, host='localhost', db='mebelix'). Note app/migrations/env.py:19 builds an identical unescaped DSN — both need the fix.

**Tekshiruv:** Parol hamon percent-encoding'siz DSN'ga qo'shiladi (config.py:126-129) — @ / kabi belgili parol bilan boot chalkash xato bilan yiqiladi. quote_plus bilan bir qatorlik arzon fix.

**Tuzatish:** Wrap the user and password in `urllib.parse.quote_plus(...)` inside the `POSTGRES_URI` computed field (or build the DSN with `sqlalchemy.engine.URL.create(...)`, which escapes components itself).

### `backend/app/core/config.py:137-150` — BUG · origin-08-29 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

_validate_production_settings refuses to boot on four placeholder secrets but not on FORWARDED_ALLOW_IPS=["*"], so a single env value silently re-opens the exact rate-limit bypass the default was changed to close — and the app-level middleware, running inside the server-level one, overrides the strict gunicorn --forwarded-allow-ips flag.

**Dalil:** def _validate_production_settings(s: Settings) -> None:

**Repro:** `ENVIRONMENT=production` + to'rt sirning hammasi haqiqiy + `FORWARDED_ALLOW_IPS='["*"]'` → `_validate_production_settings(s)` **hech narsa qaytarmaydi, boot davom etadi**. Xulq isboti: `ProxyHeadersMiddleware(app, trusted_hosts=['*'])`, XFF=`1.2.3.4, 203.0.113.9` → app `client=('1.2.3.4',0)` ko'radi; `trusted_hosts=['172.16.0.0/12']` → `('203.0.113.9',0)`. Ichma-ich (gunicorn qatlami strict, app qatlami `*`) → app oxir-oqibat `('1.2.3.4',0)` ko'radi, ya'ni app-level middleware serverdagi flagni bekor qiladi. rate_limit.py:68 kaliti `request.client.host`.

**Tekshiruv:** Validator hamon faqat 4 ta placeholder (SECRET_KEY, ADMIN_PASSWORD, POSTGRES_PASSWORD, ADMIN_PHONE) tekshiradi — `FORWARDED_ALLOW_IPS` bo'yicha tekshiruv yo'q. `.env.example:66` endi xavfsiz default (`["172.16.0.0/12"]`) ko'rsatadi, lekin bu faqat namunani o'zgartiradi — kimdir prod `.env` da `["*"]` qo'ysa validator hamon boot'ni to'xtatmaydi.

**Tuzatish:** Add one clause to _validate_production_settings: `if "*" in s.FORWARDED_ALLOW_IPS: problems.append("FORWARDED_ALLOW_IPS trusts every caller")` — it costs two lines and makes the fix from the 2026-08-26 audit unrevertable by an env file. (If setup.py:172 is dropped instead, per that audit finding, the setting and this clause both go away.)

### `backend/app/core/middleware.py:30` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

An unhandled exception in a handler bypasses the access log and the response never carries X-Request-ID, so 500s — the requests that most need correlation — are invisible in the structured log.

**Dalil:** response = await call_next(request)

**Repro:** uv run python r4_middleware.py: FastAPI app + RequestLoggingMiddleware driven via raw ASGI (TestClient unavailable: httpx2 missing), log handler captured. /ok -> status=200, X-Request-ID='rid-ok', log line 'request rid=rid-ok status=200'. /boom (RuntimeError) -> status=500, X-Request-ID=None, log=''. The exception propagates past line 31 so neither the access-log line nor the response header is produced.

**Tekshiruv:** try/except hamon yo'q (middleware.py to'liq o'qildi, 44 qator) — handler'dagi unhandled exception access-log qatorisiz va X-Request-ID header'siz o'tib ketaveradi, 500'lar strukturali logda ko'rinmaydi.

**Tuzatish:** Wrap `call_next` in try/except: on exception log the same `extra` dict with `status_code=500` and re-raise, so failures are logged with their request_id and duration.

### `backend/app/core/setup.py:78` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

The hand-rolled /openapi.json handler rebuilds the schema with `application.version` (FastAPI's hardcoded default "0.1.0") and never passes `description`, so `APP_VERSION` and the "MebeliX API" description never reach the OpenAPI document.

**Dalil:** return get_openapi(title=application.title, version=application.version, routes=application.routes)

**Repro:** uv run python r5_openapi.py: create_application(router, settings) with ENVIRONMENT=local, GET /openapi.json via raw ASGI -> info={'title':'MebeliX','version':'0.1.0'} (no description); app.openapi() gives {'title','description':'MebeliX API','version':'0.1.0'}. create_application never passes version= so app.version is FastAPI's default, equal to APP_VERSION only by coincidence. Patched get_openapi: 2 requests -> 2 calls (no caching).

**Tekshiruv:** Handler unchanged (now at line 78, one function moved above it); FastAPI(...) constructor at line 86-94 still has no version= kwarg, so application.version stays FastAPI's hardcoded default, not settings.APP_VERSION.

**Tuzatish:** Pass `version=settings.APP_VERSION` into the `kwargs.update(...)` block in `create_application` so FastAPI's `application.version` is the configured one, and change line 138 to `return application.openapi()` (which honours title/description/version and caches the schema in `application.openapi_schema` instead of regenerating it on every request).

### `backend/app/core/worker.py:20` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

Every arq log line in the worker is emitted twice — once as plain text and once as JSON — because arq's CLI applies its own dictConfig after this module has already installed the JSON root handler.

**Dalil:** setup_logging()  # arq worker entrypoint — JSON logging, xuddi app protsessidagidek

**Repro:** `uv run python` — `import app.core.worker` (setup_logging() modul darajasida) so'ng `logging.config.dictConfig(arq.logs.default_log_config(False))` (arq/cli.py 40→45 tartibi, arq 0.28.0), keyin `getLogger('arq.worker').info('test-double-log-line')`. Natija: stdout=1 JSON qator, stderr=1 plain '10:04:42: test-double-log-line', TOTAL=2; arq.propagate=True, root'da JSON handler qoladi. compose prod/local `command: arq app.core.worker.WorkerSettings` — custom-log-dict yo'q.

**Tekshiruv:** Still calls setup_logging() unconditionally and WorkerSettings carries no custom_log_dict / on_startup log override; docker-compose.local.yml:66 and prod:93 still run bare `arq app.core.worker.WorkerSettings`, so arq's own dictConfig still double-handles arq.* records against the JSON root handler.

**Tuzatish:** Pass arq a log config that does not double-handle: run the worker with `arq app.core.worker.WorkerSettings --custom-log-dict app.core.logger.ARQ_LOG_DICT` where that dict sets `{'loggers': {'arq': {'handlers': [], 'propagate': True}}}`, or drop `setup_logging()` from worker.py and let the custom log dict install JsonFormatter.

### `backend/app/migrations/script.py.mako:8` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

The revision template emits `from typing import Sequence, Union`, which fails the project's own ruff config (UP035 + I001), so every autogenerated migration must be hand-edited before it can be committed.

**Repro:** `uv run alembic revision -m proof_tmp_f07` (DB kerak emas, revision_environment=false) -> fayl 8-qator `from typing import Sequence, Union`; `uv run ruff check --no-fix <fayl>` -> exit 1: UP035 (Sequence collections.abc dan), I001 (alembic/sqlalchemy tartibi) + 2 F401 (bo'sh reviziya uchun). alembic.ini:78-81 ruff post_write_hook hamon komment. Vaqtinchalik fayl o'chirildi. Shablon 569fba5 dan beri o'zgarmagan.

**Tekshiruv:** Shablon o'zgarmagan, alembic.ini'dagi ruff post_write_hook hamon kommentda — har autogenerate'dan keyin qo'lda UP035/I001 tuzatish kerak. Bir qatorlik fix, foydasi aniq.

**Tuzatish:** Change script.py.mako line 8 to `from collections.abc import Sequence` plus `from typing import Union` in isort order (alembic import after sqlalchemy), matching e1c262d7e2ae_init.py:9-13. Optionally enable the already-present ruff post_write_hook in alembic.ini:77-81 so generated files are fixed automatically.

### `backend/app/schemas/employees.py:50` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

Update sxemalarida NOT NULL ustunlar `str | None` — explicit `null` yuborilsa route uni to'g'ridan-to'g'ri setattr qiladi va 422 o'rniga 500 chiqadi.

**Repro:** uv run python: EmployeeUpdate({'full_name':None}).model_dump(exclude_unset=True) -> {'full_name': None} (422 yo'q); ClientUpdate -> {'full_name': None}; MachineUpdate({'post_id':None,'name':None}) -> ikkalasi None. Model __table__: Employee.full_name/Client.full_name/Machine.post_id/Machine.name nullable=False. Route'lar (employees.py:111-117, clients.py:204, machines.py:125) setattr qiladi; IntegrityError faqat crud/users.py va orders/lifecycle.py da tutiladi, bu 3 route'da yo'q -> commit da 500. users.py:_drop_forbidden_nulls xuddi shu input uchun {'name': None} qaytaradi (full_name tashlanadi).

**Tekshiruv:** Himoya hamon faqat users.py da (_drop_forbidden_nulls users.py:34, boshqa hech qayerda yo'q); employees.py:115, clients.py:204, machines.py:130, posts.py:27 hamon model_dump(exclude_unset=True) natijasini to'g'ridan-to'g'ri setattr qiladi, NOT NULL ustunga explicit null 500 (IntegrityError) beradi. 422 o'rniga 500 — haqiqiy bug, tuzatish arzon (mavjud helperni umumiylashtirishning o'zi).

**Tuzatish:** `routes/users.py:34` dagi `_drop_forbidden_nulls` ni umumiy helperga chiqarib (masalan `app/api/deps.py` yoki `app/schemas/common.py`), employees/clients/machines/posts PATCH route'larida ham `model_dump(exclude_unset=True)` natijasiga qo'llang; har modul o'z `_NULLABLE_FIELDS` ro'yxatini beradi (employees: `phone`, `position`, `user_id`; clients: `phone`, `phone_alt`, `address`, `notes`; machines: hech biri).

### `backend/app/services/excel.py:52` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`_cell` `datetime` ni `TASHKENT_TZ` ga o'tkazmasdan formatlaydi, shuning uchun buyurtmalar hisobotining .xlsx eksporti UTC soatlarini (Toshkentdan 5 soat orqada) chiqaradi.

**Repro:** `uv run python`: `_cell(datetime(2026,9,2,10,0,tzinfo=UTC))` → '02.09.2026 10:00'; Toshkent kutilgan '02.09.2026 15:00'. models/base.py: ustunlar DateTime(timezone=True) → asyncpg aware-UTC qaytaradi; reports.py:210-214 created_at/production_started_at/completed_at/delivered_at ni to'g'ridan-to'g'ri xlsx_response'ga uzatadi, OrderReportRow'da serializer yo'q.

**Tekshiruv:** _cell hali ham astimezone qilmaydi; reports.py aware-UTC created_at/production_started_at/completed_at/delivered_at ni to'g'ridan-to'g'ri uzatadi, .xlsx da soatlar Toshkentdan 5 soat orqada chiqadi. Xuddi shu route qolgan joyda TASHKENT_TZ ishlatadi — foydalanuvchi ko'radigan haqiqiy xato.

**Tuzatish:** `_cell` da aware datetime'ni avval zonaga o'tkazish: `if isinstance(v, datetime): return (v.astimezone(TASHKENT_TZ) if v.tzinfo else v).strftime("%d.%m.%Y %H:%M")` (`from app.core.config import TASHKENT_TZ`).

### `backend/app/services/giblab/cutmap.py:198-204` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`rot` is computed only from the rotated-match test, so a near-square part placed UNROTATED is flagged as rotated and its kromka symbols are swapped.

**Dalil:** and abs(cell["w"] - part.width) < 1.5

**Repro:** `uv run python`: cutmap.py'dan `rot = (...)` ifodasini regex bilan olib, part.length=400,width=399 bilan eval: cell {w:400,h:399} (AYLANTIRILMAGAN) → rot=True; cell {w:399,h:400} (aylantirilgan) → rot=True. Ikkalasi ham True — unrotated holat noto'g'ri belgilanadi, _edge_markers l/r↔t/b almashtiradi.

**Tekshiruv:** rot hamon faqat almashgan-moslik testidan hisoblanadi, to'g'ridan-to'g'ri moslik (length~length, width~width) tekshirilmaydi — length≠width lekin farq <1.5mm bo'lgan aylantirilmagan detal rot=True bo'lib qoladi.

**Tuzatish:** Test the unrotated fit first and only set `rot=True` when the placed size matches the swapped pair AND not the direct pair, e.g. `fits_direct = abs(cell["w"]-part.length)<1.5 and abs(cell["h"]-part.width)<1.5; rot = not fits_direct and abs(cell["w"]-part.width)<1.5 and abs(cell["h"]-part.length)<1.5`.

### `backend/app/services/giblab/ingest.py:222-226` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

wipe_previous_parse unlinks the old .project and SCX files from disk before the ingest try/except, so a ParseError rolls the DB back to the old rows while the physical files are already gone.

**Dalil:** for path in paths:         try:             (upload_root / path).unlink(missing_ok=True)         except OSError:

**Repro:** Uchidan-uchiga repro (AsyncMock db + tempdir, files.py:83-88 ketma-ketligi aynan takrorlandi): wipe_previous_parse(db, order, root) → 'after wipe: []' (x.project va A_001.scx diskdan YO'Q); keyin ingest_project ParseError berdi ("Detal ID to'qnashuvi: A_001 ham p1, ham p2 uchun chiqdi" — ingest.py:285, wipe'dan KEYIN ishlaydigan tekshiruv); await db.rollback() → rollback awaited: 1 (DB yumshoq-o'chirish qaytdi), 'files still on disk: []'. Ya'ni DB eski qatorlarni tiklaydi, fayllar esa qaytmaydi.

**Tekshiruv:** wipe_previous_parse still physically unlinks old .project/SCX files from disk (ingest.py:222-226) before the surrounding try/except in files.py (wipe at :83, try starts :84). Disk unlinks are not part of the DB transaction, so a ParseError from ingest_project still rolls the DB soft-delete back while the files are already gone -- real data-loss bug on a failed re-upload, unchanged.

**Tuzatish:** Move the disk unlink out of wipe_previous_parse into upload_order_project after `await db.commit()` (collect the paths in the wipe, delete them only once the new parse is committed).

### `backend/app/services/giblab/ingest.py:391` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

A part that GibLab listed in the CS operation but never placed (usedCount=0) still gets an ARRA route step, so its ARRA counter can never be closed and the order can never reach COMPLETED.

**Dalil:** if pp.material_name or pp.label_num is not None:

**Repro:** uv run python scratchpad/repro2.py: t.project ga blobda YO'Q 3-detal (usedCount=0) qo'shib CS stub ro'yxatiga kiritdim → parse: `301401_01_003 label_num=3 material='LDSP OQ GLYANETS' placed=False -> ARRA step: True`. Ya'ni parser.py _parse_cs_operation har stub'ga label_num/material yozadi, build_cutmap binding bermaydi; ingest esa ARRA qadamini yaratadi, PartInstance cell_index=None bo'ladi. CutmapView.tsx:297 `if (inst.pattern_no == null) continue` — bunday nusxa xaritada tanlanmaydi/chop etilmaydi, ARRA hisoblagichi terminal oqimida yopilmaydi.

**Tekshiruv:** ARRA qadami hamon parser stub heuristikasiga asoslanadi, cutmap.bindings (haqiqiy joylashtirish)ga emas; ingest.py:472-475 dagi 'ghosts' ogohlantirishi buni tan oladi-yu, ARRA qadami baribir yaratiladi — usedCount=0 detal yopilmas ARRA hisoblagichi oladi.

**Tuzatish:** Gate the ARRA step on actual placement: `sheet_part = pp.key in bindings_by_key` when a cutmap was built (fall back to the material_name/label_num heuristic only when `cutmap is None`), so ghost parts (usedCount=0, CL profiles) get no unreachable ARRA step.

### `backend/app/services/giblab/parser.py:339` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

The documented name-as-product-code fallback can produce a value longer than the `Product.code` column, crashing ingest instead of failing with a parse message.

**Dalil:** product_code = g.get("code") or g.get("name") or "PRODUCT"

**Repro:** uv run python scratchpad/repro5.py: product code atributi olib tashlanib 72 belgili name berildi → parse_project xatosiz, `product.code len: 72 | column: VARCHAR(50)`. ingest.py:331 kesmasdan Product(code=parsed_product.code) qiladi; Product.name String(255), code String(50). Postgres varchar(50) ga 72 belgi yozish 'value too long' beradi (DB'siz shu qism bajarilmadi, lekin varchar(n) qat'iy — 500 kafolatlangan).

**Tekshiruv:** Keyingi qatorda faqat yo'l-belgisi tekshiruvi bor (340-341), uzunlik tekshiruvi yo'q; Product.code hali String(50) — 50+ belgili nom StringDataRightTruncation (500) beradi.

**Tuzatish:** In `parse_project`, right after the path-character check, validate the product code length against the column (50) — either `raise ParseError(f"Mahsulot kodi juda uzun: {product_code!r}")` or trim to 50 and append a warning, mirroring how the part-code fallback already warns.

Natija: warnings=[] , 01_001 xnc_ops=0, 01_002 xnc_ops=0 — ikkala continue (stub_el is None / not program) hamon ogohlantirishsiz. Mavjud 2 ta uploads/projects/*.project da esa har XNC opda <part> ham program ham bor (0/2 yo'qolgan).

### `backend/app/services/giblab/parser.py:452-453` — BUG · yangi sweep · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`_f()` returns `None` only when the attribute is absent, but here `or 10.0` also fires when the attribute is present and explicitly `0`. The surrounding comment says the 10.0 fallback is meant for the case the attribute is 'yozilmagan' (not written) — an explicit `cTrimL="0"` (a real, meaningful value: no trim margin) gets silently replaced with 10.0 instead of being preserved. Same pattern on the next line for `cTrimW`.

**Dalil:** trim_l=(_f(op_el, "cTrimL") or 10.0),

**Repro:** _f(<op cTrimL="0"/>, 'cTrimL') = 0.0; (0.0 or 10.0) = 10.0; atribut yo'q bo'lsa None. Ya'ni aniq yozilgan 0 ham 10.0 ga almashadi (cTrimW va kerf `or 4.4` da ham shu naqsh).

**Tekshiruv:** _f() hali aniq "0" uchun 0.0 qaytaradi (51-57), `or 10.0`/`or 4.4` hali cTrimL=0/cTrimW=0/kerf=0 holatlarini 'yozilmagan' bilan aralashtiradi. Ta'siri display-only, lekin bug o'zgarmagan.

**Tuzatish:** Use an explicit presence check instead of `or`, e.g. `v = _f(op_el, "cTrimL"); trim_l = v if v is not None else 10.0` (same for cTrimW).

### `backend/app/services/giblab/scx.py:169` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

A mill contour that contains only its `ms` head crashes `_offset_contour` with IndexError; the guard at line 169 only covers the empty-list case, which xnc.py can never produce.

**Dalil:** if not contour:

**Repro:** uv run python scratchpad/p06/r2b_scx.py — parse_xnc_program('<ms c="2"/><ml enable="false"/>') → mills=[[ms]] (len 1); _offset_contour(...) → IndexError; generate_scx(part,[op]) → IndexError (comp="0" yo'li esa OK). Chaqiruvchi ingest.py:250 build_scx_files try/except'siz → ingest 500.

**Tekshiruv:** Guard hali ham faqat bo'sh ro'yxatni ushlaydi; xnc.py:225-235 <ms> ni darhol mills ga append qiladi, keyingi ml/mac enable=false bo'lsa (208-qatordagi continue) 1 elementli kontur qoladi -> scx.py 306-309 dagi segs bo'sh -> 350-qatorda out_pts[0]=seg_pts[0][0] IndexError.

**Tuzatish:** Replace `if not contour: continue` with `if len(contour) < 2: continue` (a single point produces no `<Line>` elements anyway), or make `_offset_contour` return `(head_x, head_y, [])` when `segs` is empty.

### `backend/app/services/giblab/xnc.py:136` — BUG · yangi sweep · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`_groove()` xujjatlaydi (comment) va spesifikatsiya bo'yicha `gr` tegining `c` (korreksiya) atributi 1 yoki 2 bo'lsa paz o'q bo'ylab t/2 ga suriladigan bo'lishi kerak, lekin kod `el.get("c")`ni umuman o'qimaydi va qaytarilgan dict'da yo'q — hech qanday tuzatish qo'llanmaydi. Solishtirish uchun: `ms` tegi xuddi shu `c` atributini o'qiydi va saqlaydi (`"c": el.get("c")`, 231-qator), keyin `scx.py`da haqiqatan HAM ofset sifatida qo'llaniladi (`_offset_contour`, scx.py:194-197). Grooves uchun bunday zanjir yo'q — na xnc.py, na scx.py hech qayerda `c`ni o'qimaydi. Korpusdagi barcha fayllarda c=0 bo'lgani uchun hozircha sezilmayapti, lekin c=1/2 li fayl kelsa paz koordinatasi jimgina (ogohlantirishsiz) noto'g'ri chiqadi — bu fayl o'zining boshqa joyida amal qiladigan "hech narsa jim yo'qolmaydi" tamoyiliga zid.

**Dalil:** def _groove(el: ET.Element, num: _Num) -> dict[str, Any]:

**Repro:** parse_xnc_program('P1', '<program dx=600 dy=400 dz=16><gr ... c="{c}"/></program>') c=0/1/2 uchun — uchala natija bir xil dict {x1,y1,x2,y2,dp,t}, 'c' kaliti yo'q; inspect: scx.py grooves uchun c ni o'qimaydi (doim ToolOffset='中'). c=1/2 jim e'tiborsiz qoladi.

**Tekshiruv:** _groove() hali `c` atributini o'qimaydi/qaytarmaydi (faqat `ms`/mill uchun 231-qatorda `"c": el.get("c")` bor). c=1/2 li paz hali jimgina noto'g'ri chiqadi.

**Tuzatish:** Yo `_groove()`da `c` atributini o'qib t/2 siljishni qo'llash (ms/mill uchun qilingani kabi), yoki hech bo'lmasa `c not in (None, "0")` bo'lsa ParseError/warning berish — hozirgi kabi jimgina noto'g'ri geometriya chiqarish o'rniga.

### `backend/app/services/metrics.py:27` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`part_volume` `Post.unit` ni o'qimaydi: UPAKOVKA posti birligi `m2` bo'lsa ham dona qaytaradi, natijada TABLO va dashboard "m²" yorlig'i ostida DONA sonini ko'rsatadi.

**Dalil:** return float(count)

**Repro:** uv run python scratchpad/p08/r2_metrics.py: seed POSTS[UPAKOVKA].unit = m2; part_volume('UPAKOVKA', 1000x500 mm, count=3) = 3.0 (dona), ARRA bir xil kirish bilan = 1.5 (m2). tablo.py:216 va reports.py:358 birlikni post.unit dan oladi, frontend tablo.tsx:476 `UNIT_LABELS[p.unit]` ni ustun sarlavhasida chiqaradi -> "m²" ostida dona soni. Seed izohi (seed_defaults.py:72-73) ish haqi kvadratura bo'yicha deydi.

**Tekshiruv:** UPAKOVKA hali ham ARRA/KROMKA maxsus shoxlariga tushmay float(count) qaytaradi; seed_defaults.py:78 UPAKOVKA'ni PostUnit.M2 qilib seedlaydi, reports.py/tablo.py post.unit'dan 'm²' yorlig'ini oladi — ekranda m² ostida dona chiqadi.

Agar approksimatsiya ataylab bo'lsa, `seed_defaults` da UPAKOVKA birligini `PostUnit.PIECE` ga tushirish, aks holda ekranda "m²" yozib dona ko'rsatiladi.

### `backend/app/services/scanning.py:651-660` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`record_scan_at` dagi `except IntegrityError` bloki erishib bo'lmaydigan — poyga 422 emas, 500 beradi.

**Repro:** uv run python scratchpad/p08/r4_autoflush.py: sqlite in-memory + default sessionmaker (autoflush=True, core/db.py:28 async_sessionmaker ham default) bilan aynan record_scan_at naqshi: add(Scan dup client_event_id) -> execute(update Route) -> try: flush. Natija: `IntegrityError execute(update) DA otdi (try/flush'dan OLDIN): UNIQUE constraint failed: scan.client_event_id`. scanning.py:644 db.add, :648 execute(update), :650 try — tuzilish shu; app'da IntegrityError uchun global exception_handler yo'q (grep bo'sh) -> 500.

**Tekshiruv:** Tuzilish o'zgarmagan: db.add(scan) (651) -> execute(update PartRoute) (656) -> refresh (657) -> try/flush (658-660). local_session hali autoflush=False bermaydi (db.py:26), ya'ni 656-execute kutayotgan Scan INSERT'ini o'zi flush qiladi va IntegrityError try'dan tashqarida ko'tariladi.

**Tuzatish:** `try` blokini `db.add(scan)` dan boshlab 591-592 qatorlarni ham ichiga olish, yoki `db.execute(...).execution_options(synchronize_session=False)` o'rniga avval `await db.flush()` ni chaqirib, keyin UPDATE yuborish.

## frontend

### `frontend/nginx/index.conf:19` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`try_files` oxirida `=404` turgani uchun `/index.html` ichki redirect emas, FAYL tekshiruvi bo'ladi — SPA chuqur havolalari (`/orders/123`) `location = /index.html` blokidan o'tmaydi va `Cache-Control: no-cache` header'ini OLMAYDI

**Dalil:** try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html =404;

**Repro:** Lokal nginx 1.24 ni ikki konf bilan ko'tardim (=404 li va =404 siz), curl -i /orders/123. BUG konf: 200 + index.html tanasi, Cache-Control YO'Q (/ va /index.html da no-cache bor). FIX konf (try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html;): /orders/123 -> Cache-Control: no-cache. Da'vo aynan tasdiqlandi: chuqur havolalar location = /index.html blokidan o'tmaydi.

**Tekshiruv:** try_files hali oxirida =404 bilan tugaydi — /index.html shu tufayli ichki redirect emas, fayl sifatida joriy location / kontekstida beriladi, location = /index.html dagi no-cache header chuqur havolalarga (masalan /orders/123) qo'llanmaydi.

**Tuzatish:** `try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html;` ga o'zgartirish (oxirgi parametr URI bo'lsa nginx ichki redirect qiladi va `location = /index.html` bloki, ya'ni no-cache, chuqur havolalarga ham qo'llanadi). Zaruriy bo'lsa `root /usr/share/nginx/html;` ni `server` darajasiga ko'tarib ikki blokdagi takrorni ham yo'q qilish.

### `frontend/src/components/Anketa/RoomPhotos.tsx:70,165` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

Klient MIME tekshiruvi serverdan kengroq: `image/*` o'tadi, lekin server faqat jpeg/png/webp qabul qiladi — iPhone'ning HEIC surati (yoki GIF/SVG) klientdan o'tib serverda 422 bo'ladi.

**Dalil:** if (!f.type.startsWith("image/")) {

**Repro:** node -e: image/heic, image/heif, image/gif, image/svg+xml uchun f.type.startsWith('image/') = true, server to'plami {jpeg,png,webp} (survey.py:75 _ROOM_PHOTO_TYPES, :105 da 422) = false. Klient filtri :70 va accept='image/*' :166 hali ham o'zgarmagan; :197 yordam matni 'JPG, PNG, WEBP' va'da qiladi.

**Tekshiruv:** Klient hali ham image/* qabul qiladi (70-qator tekshiruv, 165-qator accept="image/*"), server esa faqat jpeg/png/webp — iPhone HEIC klientdan o'tib serverda 422 bo'ladi.

**Tuzatish:** Klientda ham aniq ro'yxat: `const ACCEPTED = ["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp"]`, `accept={ACCEPTED.join(",")}` va `if (!ACCEPTED.includes(f.type)) toast.error(`${f.name}: faqat JPG, PNG yoki WEBP`)` — serverga bormasdan tushunarli xabar (komponent izohi :65 aynan shuni va'da qiladi).

### `frontend/src/components/Clients/AddClient.tsx:158` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`address` has a 255-char zod limit but no `FormMessage` and no `maxLength`, so an over-long address silently blocks submit with no visible reason.

**Repro:** node + zod 4.5.4: formSchema.safeParse({full_name:'Ali Valiyev', address:'x'.repeat(256)}) -> valid:false, 'address: Too big: expected string to have <=255 characters'. awk bilan address FormItem (:187-198) ichida FormMessage soni = 0, faylda maxLength = 0. handleSubmit xato bo'lsa onValid'ni chaqirmaydi -> Saqlash jim bloklanadi (faqat ui/input.tsx:13 aria-invalid qizil ramka, matn yo'q).

**Tekshiruv:** Zod limiti (:35) turibdi, address FormItem (:187-198) esa hali ham FormMessage'siz va maxLength'siz (notes ham shunday); full_name/phone/phone_alt larda FormMessage bor. 255+ belgili manzilda submit sababsiz jim bloklanadi — Saqlash o'lik ko'rinadi. Ikki qatorlik haqiqiy UX tuzatish.

**Tuzatish:** Add `<FormMessage />` to the address (and notes) FormItem and put `maxLength={255}` on the address Input.

### `frontend/src/components/Clients/ClientCombobox.tsx:37` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

The visible label is never derived from `value`, so the trigger can show "Mijozni tanlang" while the form still holds a selected client id.

**Repro:** jsdom + esbuild bilan HAQIQIY ClientCombobox va ui/dialog (Radix) ni AddOrder'dagidek useForm ichida render qildim: (1) dialog ochildi -> trigger 'Mijozni tanlang'; (2) form.client_id='7' -> trigger hamon 'Mijozni tanlang' (label value'dan chiqarilmaydi); (3) dialog yopildi -> combobox UNMOUNT bo'ldi, client_id='7' qoldi (form.reset faqat onSuccess :61); (4) qayta ochildi -> client_id='7', trigger 'Mijozni tanlang'. orderForm.tsx:30 min(1) o'tadi -> submit ko'rinmas mijozga buyurtma yaratadi.

**Tekshiruv:** O'zgarmagan: label faqat initialLabel/select() dan keladi, value'dan hech qachon chiqarilmaydi; AddOrder initialLabel bermaydi va form.reset() faqat onSuccess'da (:61), dialog yopilishida emas. Dialog yopib-ochilsa client_id saqlangan, combobox esa 'Mijozni tanlang' ko'rsatadi — menejer ko'rinmas mijozga buyurtma yaratishi mumkin. Haqiqiy footgun, tuzatish kerak (label'ni value'dan chiqarish yoki yopilishda reset).

**Tuzatish:** Derive the label from `value`: keep a `selected` map/lookup (or reset `label` when `value` is empty via an effect), and have AddOrder call `form.reset()` in `onOpenChange` when the dialog closes.

### `frontend/src/components/Common/BadgePrompt.tsx:66-77` — BUG · origin-08-29 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`pathLength={1}` is set on the `<g>` wrapper instead of on the two `<rect>` children, so the badge card outline never draws itself — it renders as a permanent 1-unit dotted rectangle, exactly the dashed look the file's own comment says was deliberately removed.

**Dalil:** pathLength={1}

**Repro:** Rendered the exact markup + the verbatim `.mx-draw` CSS (index.css:247-251) in headless Chrome, rasterised to canvas and counted ink along the card's top edge. `google-chrome --headless=new --dump-dom .../badge.html` (animation END, dashoffset:0):   g ctor=SVGGElement, 'pathLength' in g ? **false**  |  rect ctor=SVGRectElement, 'pathLength' in rect? true   rect getTotalLength() = 478.4 ; computed stroke-dasharray on rect (inherited) = 1px   BUGGY (pathLength on <g>)    top edge: ink=200 px, **50 separate dashes**   FIXED (pathLength on <rect>) top edge: ink=395 px, 2 runs (solid) Same page at animation START (dashoffset:1): BUGGY ink=199 / 49 dashes (IDENTICAL to the end state — it never animates), FIXED ink=2 (invisible → then draws to solid).

**Tekshiruv:** pathLength={1} hali <g> o'ramida, ikkita <rect> bolada emas; SVGGElement'da bu atribut yo'q (faqat SVGGeometryElement'da), shuning uchun .mx-draw dagi stroke-dasharray:1/stroke-dashoffset:1 normallanmagan holda merosga o'tadi va ramka doimiy 1-birlik nuqtali chiziq bo'lib qoladi — fayl o'zi rad etgan 'punktir ramka' effekti.

**Tuzatish:** Move `pathLength={1}` off the `<g>` and onto each `<rect>`: `<rect x="5" y="5" width="110" height="138" rx="10" pathLength={1} />` and the same on the lanyard-slot rect.

### `frontend/src/components/Common/ErrorComponent.tsx:3` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

The router's global errorComponent throws away TanStack Router's `error` and `reset` props, so every uncaught render/loader error shows one identical generic sentence with no detail and no retry path.

**Repro:** npx tsx (react-dom/server + createRouter isServer, loader throw): ROOT route xatosi -> `() => <ErrorComponent/>` faqat 'Xatolik yuz berdi...' chiqaradi, props-aware variant esa error.message ('DB 500: buyurtma topilmadi') ni oladi. QO'SHIMCHA: CHILD route xatosi (real hayotdagi deyarli hammasi) custom ErrorComponent'ga umuman yetmaydi — TanStack'ning inglizcha built-in 'Something went wrong! / Show Error' chiqadi, chunki main.tsx createRouter'da defaultErrorComponent yo'q va faqat __root.tsx:51 errorComponent bor (Match.js:39 route.options.errorComponent ?? router.options.defaultErrorComponent).

**Tekshiruv:** Komponent hali ham propssiz, __root.tsx:51 `errorComponent: () => <ErrorComponent />` bilan error/reset tashlanadi — har uncaught xato bir xil umumiy matn, retry yo'li yo'q. extractErrorMessage + reset qo'shish kichik va foydali.

**Tuzatish:** Give ErrorComponent an `({ error, reset })` signature; render `extractErrorMessage(error)` (already used in routes/login.tsx:140, 256) under the generic line and add a "Qayta urinish" button calling `reset()`. Wire it as `errorComponent: ErrorComponent` so the router's props actually arrive.

The "Bekor qilish" button resets only `draft`, leaving unsaved `parents` (assembly-parent) selections in place — the button disappears (dirty flips false only if draft was the sole edit) while the parent choice silently survives into the next save.

**Tekshiruv:** Bekor qilish hali ham faqat setDraft({}) — parents (asosiy detal tanlovlari) jim saqlanib keyingi saqlashga kirib ketadi; success yo'li (L70-71) ikkalasini ham tozalaydi. Bir qatorli tuzatish, haqiqiy xulq nuqsoni.

**Tuzatish:** `onClick={() => { setDraft({}); setParents({}) }}` — same reset the success handler already performs.

**Dalil:** ? `Nostandart · ${part.routes.length} qadam`

**Repro:** uv run python: _set_custom_route(fake db, codes=[1,5], packaging=True) -> db.add: [(1,1),(2,5),(3,15),(4,16)] — dum har doim qo'shiladi; read_order_matrix (matrix.py:37-51) faqat is_deleted bo'yicha filtrlaydi, is_enabled/TAIL filtri yo'q. npx tsx: badge = 'Nostandart · 4 qadam', texnolog chizgani 2. QO'SHIMCHA (yomonroq): CustomRoutes.tsx:87-88 codesOf ham dumni oladi -> sarlavha '1 / 5 / 15 / 16', toggle'da oxirgi chizilgan qadamni bekor qilib bo'lmaydi (lastIndexOf != length-1) va saqlashda route_codes [1,5,15,16,3] ketadi; backend uni qabul qiladi (15/16 PROCESS_POSTS da bor) -> 7 qadam: [(1,1),(2,5),(3,15),(4,16),(5,3),(6,15),(7,16)] — UPAKOVKA/TMO marshrut o'rtasida takrorlanadi.

**Tekshiruv:** Backend hali ham UPAKOVKA/TMO dum qadamlarini har doim qo'shadi (matrix.py:389-390) va read_order_matrix is_enabled bo'yicha filtrlamaydi; badge xom part.routes.length ni sanaydi — N 1-2 ga oshirilgan.

**Tuzatish:** Count only the steps the technologist owns: `part.routes.filter(r => r.is_enabled && !TAIL_CODES.includes(r.process_code)).length`, sharing the same TAIL_CODES constant as the CustomRoutes fix.

### `frontend/src/components/Orders/OrderMatrix.tsx:265` — BUG · origin-08-29 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

The note field is uncontrolled (`defaultValue`), so filtering the search box while in edit mode unmounts and remounts the row and the input snaps back to the server value — while `noteEdits` still holds (and will silently save) the typed text.

""}` + `onChange` into `noteEdits`) and ran it: `node .../matrix.cjs` →   after typing   : DOM = "QO'LDA YOZILGAN" | noteEdits = {"1":"QO'LDA YOZILGAN"}   filtered out   : row1 in DOM? false   after refilter : DOM = **"SERVER-NOTE"** | noteEdits = {"1":"QO'LDA YOZILGAN"}   SAVED value would be: "QO'LDA YOZILGAN"  ->  MISMATCH: true Save path verified at OrderMatrix.tsx:83 (`...Object.keys(noteEdits)`) and :92 (`note: noteEdits[partId] ?? null`).

**Tekshiruv:** Izoh input'i hali uncontrolled (defaultValue); noteEdits state'i mavjud bo'lsa ham value={noteEdits[part.id] ?? part.note ?? ""} ga o'zgartirilmagan — filtr bilan qator qayta-mount bo'lganda kiritilgan matn ko'rinishda yo'qoladi, lekin noteEdits state'ida qolib saqlanadi.
