### `frontend/src/components/Orders/OrderMatrix.tsx:46` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

OrderMatrix and CustomRoutes destructure only `data`/`isLoading` from the matrix query, so a failed request renders a misleading "no parts yet" empty state instead of an error — there is no global query error toast to fall back on.

**Dalil:** const { data: matrix, isLoading } = useQuery(

### `frontend/src/components/People/PersonDialog.tsx:142` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

Phone is turned into a payload by stripping non-digits with no `PHONE_PATTERN` check, so a half-typed number is sent as e.g. `+99890` and the server 422s.

**Dalil:** const digits = form.phone.replace(/\D/g, "")

**Repro:** grep: PersonDialog.tsx:137-138 `const digits = form.phone.replace(/\D/g, ""); const phone = digits ? `+${digits}` : null`, L244-257 `missing` faqat `!phone` ni tekshiradi. npx tsx: '+998 90' -> payload '+99890', missing bloklamaydi, PHONE_PATTERN false; paste '901234567' -> ekranda '+998 90 123-45-67' ko'rinadi, payload '+901234567' (PATTERN false). uv run python: validate_phone('+99890') va ('+901234567') -> ValueError 'Telefon +998 bilan boshlanib, 9 ta raqamdan iborat bo'lsin' (422).

**Tekshiruv:** Hali ham faqat raqam-stripping, formattedPhoneSchema tekshiruvi yo'q; missing hisobida ham format tekshiruvi yo'q (faqat !phone tekshiriladi) — chala telefon serverga boradi.

**Tuzatish:** Validate before enabling Saqlash: `const parsed = formattedPhoneSchema.safeParse(form.phone)` and use `parsed.data` as the canonical phone, adding a "Telefon +998XXXXXXXXX formatida" entry to `missing`.

### `frontend/src/components/People/PersonDialog.tsx:146-150` — BUG · yangi sweep · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`switching` (which triggers the destructive-change confirm dialog) checks only whether the ORIGINAL person had any linked user account, not whether the ofis/tsex kind actually changed. A tsex worker with a personal shop login (`access: "both"`) always has `person.user` set. Editing such a person while leaving their position as tsex (kind stays "tsex", shopLogin still checked) still makes `kind !== "ofis" && !!person?.user` true, so `switching` is true even though nothing is being switched — every edit of a "both access" employee (e.g. just changing the phone number) pops the 'Tsexga o'tkazilsinmi?' confirm dialog claiming 'kirish hisobi o'chiriladi' (login will be deleted), which is false since the mutation's actual delete condition (`!account && userId !== null`) is not met.

**Dalil:** ((kind === "ofis" && !!person?.employee) ||       (kind !== "ofis" && !!person?.user))

**Repro:** grep -F bilan 145 va 227-qator ifodalari tasdiqlandi; node'da so'zma-so'z ifodalar: person={employee, user(role arrachi)}, kind='tsex', shopLogin=true, chosen.role='arrachi' → switching=true ("Tsexga o'tkazilsinmi? ... kirish hisobi o'chiriladi" dialogi, :404-409), deleteUser sharti `!account && userId!==null && canManageAccounts`=false. employees.tsx:171 user_id'li hodimga user to'ldiradi, positionOf byRole → Arrachi (tsex).

**Tekshiruv:** switching hisoblash hali o'zgarmagan — shaxsiy loginli (access=both) tsex xodimini oddiy tahrirlashda ham 'Tsexga o'tkazilsinmi?' dialogi noto'g'ri chiqadi.

**Tuzatish:** Compute whether the person was ORIGINALLY ofis from data (`!person?.employee && !!person?.user`, i.e. a user with no employee record), and compare that to the current `kind` instead of the raw `!!person?.user` presence check: `const originallyOfis = !person?.employee && !!person?.user; const switching = editing && ((kind === "ofis" && !originallyOfis) || (kind !== "ofis" && originallyOfis))`. This fires the confirm only on an actual ofis<->tsex switch, not whenever an already-tsex employee with a personal login is re-saved unchanged.

### `frontend/src/components/People/PersonDialog.tsx:227` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

User account and employee row are created in one non-atomic mutationFn; if the employee step fails, the created account is orphaned, invisible in the list, and a retry fails on duplicate phone.

**Dalil:** await EmployeesService.createEmployee({ body })

**Repro:** mutationFn matnini fayldan ajratib (typescript.transpileModule) soxta UsersService/EmployeesService bilan ishga tushirdim: kind=tsex, withLogin, person=null, createEmployee reject → calls=[createUser(+998901234567), createEmployee], deleteUser chaqirilmadi (false), mutation reject. Hozirgi kod L187 createUser → L222 createEmployee, try/catch yo'q; yagona deleteUser (L228) `!account` sharti ostida. Tsex roli (kromkachi...) OPERATOR_ROLES∈DEVICE_ROLES → employees.tsx:196 `isDeviceAccount(u) || linked.has(...)` yetim hisobni yashiradi; qayta urinish users.py:68 `crud_users.exists(phone)` → 409 'Bu telefon allaqachon'. deleteUser UI faqat ro'yxatdagi qatorlarda — yashirin yetimni admin o'chira olmaydi.

**Tekshiruv:** createUser (191-200) va createEmployee (227) orasida rollback yo'q; employees.tsx:197 isDeviceAccount(u)||linked.has(...) yetim hisobni yashiradi, qayta urinish esa duplikat telefon 409 iga uriladi.

**Tuzatish:** Either create the employee first and patch `user_id` afterwards, or wrap the failure path in a compensating `UsersService.deleteUser(created.id)` before rethrowing; a backend endpoint that does both in one transaction is the real fix.

### `frontend/src/components/Sidebar/AppSidebar.tsx:76` — HUJJAT DRIFTI · eski (hujjat) · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

The docs (and the code comment they mirror) claim the badge query adds no request because the registry page dedupes it. That only holds ON `/orders`. The sidebar is mounted by `_layout` on EVERY admin page, and the query carries `refetchInterval: 30_000`, so on /clients, /employees, /work-centers, /reports, /logs, /printers, /settings, /anketa/:id, /cutmap/:id and `/` the sidebar is the sole subscriber and drives a 30-second poll of `/orders/status-counts` all on its own. The docs are wrong.

**Dalil:** // Reyestr sahifasi ham AYNAN shu kalitni ishlatadi — TanStack dedupe qiladi,   // ya'ni sidebar qo'shimcha so'rov qo'shmaydi.

**Repro:** 1) Kim obuna: `grep -rn getOrderStatusCountsQueryOptions src/` -> faqat AppSidebar.tsx:80 va routes/_layout/orders/index.tsx:112. `_layout.tsx:42` da `<AppSidebar />` shartsiz chiziladi, `find src/routes/_layout -type f` -> 13 sahifa, ya'ni 12 tasida sidebar yolg'iz obunachi. 2) query-core 5.102.8 bilan BAJARILGAN repro (scratchpad/poll4.mjs, node, globalThis.window bilan isServer=false): opts={queryKey:["orders","status-counts"], refetchInterval:100} -> "A: yolg'iz sidebar observer, 550ms/100ms -> 6 fetch", "B: sidebar unmount -> qo'shimcha 0 fetch", "C: sidebar + reyestr (2 observer) -> 6 fetch". Ya'ni dedupe 1 va 2 obunachida BIR XIL sonni beradi — yolg'iz sidebar pollni to'liq o'zi haydaydi. 3) `grep -Fn "sidebar qo'shimcha so'rov qo'shmaydi"` -> AppSidebar.tsx:77 va docs/06-frontend-components.md:358 (ikkalasi ham shartsiz). docs/05-frontend-app.md:336 esa rostini yozgan: "refetchInterval 30s — reestr tab'lari + sidebar badge" — hujjatlar bir-biriga zid.

**Tekshiruv:** Kod izohi (AppSidebar.tsx:76-77) va docs/06:358 ikkalasi ham hali shartsiz 'sidebar qo'shimcha so'rov qo'shmaydi' deydi; aslida dedupe faqat /orders sahifasida ishlaydi, boshqa admin sahifalarda (getOrderStatusCountsQueryOptions refetchInterval 30_000, queries/mes.ts:122) sidebar yolg'iz obunachi bo'lib mustaqil 30s poll yuritadi. Eski audit tuzatishi bu daraxtda yo'q.

### `frontend/src/components/Sidebar/Main.tsx:43` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

Active-item detection is a strict equality on the full pathname, so on any child route the sidebar shows NO highlighted item — the user loses their place in the navigation.

**Repro:** node -e: "/orders/123" === "/orders" → false; fix bilan startsWith("/orders/") → true. Main.tsx:43 `const isActive = currentPath === item.path` yagona faollik manbai (RouterLink'ga activeProps berilmagan); routes/_layout/orders/$orderId.tsx mavjud, AppSidebar:44 path="/orders".

**Tekshiruv:** Qat'iy tenglik o'z joyida — /orders/123 (routes/_layout/orders/$orderId.tsx) da hech bir band aktiv bo'lmaydi, foydalanuvchi navigatsiyada o'rnini yo'qotadi. Prefix-match (root uchun istisno bilan) — kichik, real UX tuzatish.

**Tuzatish:** Special-case root and prefix-match the rest: `const isActive = item.path === "/" ? currentPath === "/" : currentPath === item.path || currentPath.startsWith(`${item.path}/`)`. Plain `startsWith` alone is wrong here because `/` would then match every route.

**Tekshiruv:** Hali faqat aniq (===) solishtirish; /orders/$orderId kabi ichki marshrutlarda sidebar band faol ko'rinmaydi.

**Tuzatish:** startsWith mantig'i qo'sh: masalan `currentPath === item.path || (item.path !== "/" && currentPath.startsWith(item.path + "/"))`, shunda /orders/123 ham "Buyurtmalar" bandini faol qiladi.

### `frontend/src/components/Terminal/audio.ts:6` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

The lazily created `AudioContext` is never `resume()`d, so on the WS-driven (stanok agent) scan path — which by design involves no interaction with that browser — the context stays suspended and the mandatory audio signal is silent.

**Repro:** audio.ts ni bandl qilib globalThis.AudioContext ni state='suspended', resume() hisoblagichli soxta klass bilan almashtirdim; playSuccess()+playError()+playWarning() -> created=1, resume() calls=0. grep -c resume audio.ts = 0. Kontekstning brauzerda haqiqatan suspended qolishi bu yerda BAJARILMADI — Chrome autoplay hujjati (gesture'siz yaratilgan ctx suspended tug'iladi) asosida.

**Tekshiruv:** beep() da ctx.resume()/state tekshiruvi hali ham yo'q; WS-stanok yo'li (routes/terminal.tsx:153 izohi, :201/215-216 play* chaqiruvlari) foydalanuvchi interaksiyasisiz ishlaydi — suspended kontekstda majburiy signal jim qoladi. Bir qatorlik standart fix (if suspended -> resume). Ijro bilan isbotlanmagan, lekin brauzer semantikasi hujjatlashtirilgan va kodda resume yo'qligi fakt.

**Tuzatish:** In `beep`, after `ctx ??= new AudioContext()`, add `if (ctx.state === "suspended") ctx.resume()` (and optionally create/resume the context once on the first `keydown`/`pointerdown` in `useKiosk`).

### `frontend/src/components/Terminal/useTerminalScan.ts:105` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

On KROMKA/OVAL the cooldown map is only written on the success paths, so a rejected QR bypasses the 5s barrier entirely and every repeat read fires another `resolveTerminalQr` request plus another toast.

**Repro:** Bandl qilingan useTerminalScan, postCode=KROMKA, mapOrderId=1, kromkaScanRef -> 'NOTFOUND': handler('QR-X') x3 ketma-ket -> resolveTerminalQr calls=3, audio=['error','error','error'], toast=3 ta, onScan=0. err='matn' bilan x3 -> 3 toast, 3 beep. Nazorat: qabul qilingan QR x2 -> onScan=1, ikkinchisi '5 soniyadan keyin' to'sig'iga uradi — to'siq faqat muvaffaqiyatda yoziladi (:128, :137).

**Tekshiruv:** KROMKA/OVAL da cooldown xaritasi hali ham faqat muvaffaqiyatda yoziladi (:130 va :139/.then ichida); NOTFOUND (107-123) va err (125-128) yo'llari set'siz return qiladi — rad etilgan QR ning har takror o'qilishi yangi resolveTerminalQr so'rovi + yangi toast. Skaner ikki marta o'qishi odatiy; to'siq tekshiruvidan keyin darhol set qilish bir qatorlik fix.

**Tuzatish:** Record `cooldownRef.current.set(value, Date.now())` immediately after the 5s barrier check passes (before calling `kromkaScanRef.current`), so every attempt — accepted or rejected — starts the barrier, and drop the now-redundant writes at 130/139.

### `frontend/src/components/UserSettings/UserInformation.tsx:100-107` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

Clearing the name field silently does nothing: the cleared value becomes `undefined`, is dropped from the JSON body, yet a success toast fires and the old name reappears after invalidation.

Backend: uv run python — UserUpdate({}).model_dump(exclude_unset) -> {} (hech narsa yangilanmaydi), UserUpdate({name:null}) -> {'name': None} va _drop_forbidden_nulls uni saqlaydi ('name' _NULLABLE_FIELDS da, User.name Mapped[str|None]). Kod hozir ham L71-72 `updateData.name = data.name`, toast.success L58 shartsiz.

### `frontend/src/lib/mes.ts:215, routes/terminal.tsx:60,75, routes/_layout.tsx:30` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`OPERATOR_ROLES` is an `as const` tuple, which forces every call site to cast the argument with `as never` — silently disabling exactly the rename-safety the file's own comment promises.

**Dalil:** OPERATOR_ROLES.includes(me.role as never)

**Repro:** tsc repro (frontend/node_modules/.bin/tsc --noEmit --strict). a.ts = current shape (`const OPERATOR_ROLES = ["arrachi",...] as const` + `.includes(me.role as never)`) against a UserRole where "arrachi" was renamed to "arrachiy" -> exit=0, NO error. b.ts = proposed shape (`const OPERATOR_ROLES: readonly UserRole[] = [...]`, no cast) -> `error TS2820: Type '"arrachi"' is not assignable to type 'UserRole'. Did you mean '"arrachiy"'?`, exit=2. grep confirms the live shape: mes.ts:29 TERMINAL_STATUSES `as const`, :181 MACHINE_BOUND_ROLES `as const`, :220 OPERATOR_ROLES `as const`; casts at _layout.tsx:30 `as never`, terminal.tsx:60 `as never`, terminal.tsx:75 `as never`, AppSidebar.tsx:85 `as readonly string[]`, $orderId.tsx:193 `as readonly string[]` (5 casts, not 6 — employees.tsx:110 spreads without a cast). Cost of the fix also measured: fix.ts models all 6 call sites under the proposed typing -> only 2 errors remain, terminal.tsx:75 (`currentUser?.role` is `UserRole | undefined`) and AppSidebar.tsx:85 (`Object.entries(counts)` key is `string`), so 4/6 sites become cast-free outright and 2 need a one-token tweak.

**Tekshiruv:** OPERATOR_ROLES/TERMINAL_STATUSES/MACHINE_BOUND_ROLES hali `as const` tuple (mes.ts:24,176,215), uchala chaqiruv joyida `as never`/`as readonly string[]` cast hali bor — rename-xavfsizligi hali o'chirilgan.

**Tuzatish:** Declare `export const OPERATOR_ROLES: readonly UserRole[] = [...]` (and `TERMINAL_STATUSES: readonly OrderStatus[]`, `MACHINE_BOUND_ROLES: readonly UserRole[]`), then delete the `as never` / `as readonly string[]` casts at all six call sites so a rename breaks the build as intended.

### `frontend/src/lib/queryClient.ts:23-26` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

The single QueryClient declares no `defaultOptions`, so every query in the app inherits TanStack v5 defaults `staleTime: 0`, `refetchOnWindowFocus: true` and `retry: 3` — the heaviest endpoints refetch on every alt-tab, and every real error takes ~7s of silent retries before `isError` turns true.

**Dalil:** export const queryClient = new QueryClient({

**Repro:** node + query-core (window stub bilan brauzer rejimi), QueryObserver (useQuery bilan bir xil) yiqiluvchi queryFn: 4 urinish, 7014 ms keyin isError; getDefaultOptions()={}, retry/staleTime/refetchOnWindowFocus undefined (retryer.js:89 `retry ?? 3`, shouldFetchOn: undefined→refetch). Eslatma: fetchQuery retry=false qiladi (queryClient.js:161), shuning uchun faqat useQuery'ga tegishli. Terminal ko'rinishlari refetchInterval 30-60s bilan o'zlari boshqaradi.

**Tekshiruv:** QueryClient hali defaultOptions bermaydi — queryCache/mutationCache'dan boshqa hech narsa yo'q, retry:3/staleTime:0/refetchOnWindowFocus:true global default bo'lib qoladi.

**Tuzatish:** Add `defaultOptions: { queries: { staleTime: 30_000, retry: 1, refetchOnWindowFocus: false } }` to the QueryClient and drop the now-redundant per-consumer overrides. The retry default matters most: it silently multiplies the latency of every already-reported "error is swallowed" finding by ~7s.

### `frontend/src/lib/scxDir.ts:89-91` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`pickDir` swallows every error as "user cancelled", so a real failure (SecurityError outside a user gesture, NotAllowedError) makes the "Hammasini yuklab olish" button do nothing at all — no toast, no error.

**Dalil:** return null // foydalanuvchi bekor qildi

**Repro:** pickDir so'zma-so'z ko'chirilib tsx'da window.showDirectoryPicker stub bilan chaqirildi: AbortError→null, SecurityError→null, NotAllowedError→null, TypeError→null — hammasi bir xil. PrisadkaView:153 `if (!dir) return` (finally progress=null) va :196 `if (dir) toast` — null'da hech qanday xabar yo'q.

**Tekshiruv:** pickDir hali har qanday xatoni (NotAllowedError, SecurityError va h.k.) 'bekor qildi' deb bir xil catch{} bilan yutadi, AbortError'dan ajratilmagan.

**Tuzatish:** Catch the error object and re-throw / return a discriminated result for anything whose `name !== "AbortError"`, then surface it with `toast.error` in PrisadkaView so the operator knows why nothing happened.

### `frontend/src/lib/validation.ts:24-26, backend/app/schemas/users.py:35` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

The "special character" rule differs between the two sides: `ʻ` and `ʼ` (the Uzbek oʻ/gʻ apostrophes) satisfy zod but not the server, so a password the form accepts is rejected with 422.

**Dalil:** if not any(not c.isalnum() for c in v):

**Repro:** `uv run python -c` bilan validate_password_strength('Parolim1ʻ') → ValueError "Parolda kamida bitta belgi bo'lsin" (ʻ/ʼ = kategoriya Lm, isalnum()=True); `node -e` bilan /[^a-zA-Z0-9]/.test('Parolim1ʻ') → true. Ya'ni zod o'tkazadi, server 422 beradi. Backend xabari endi o'zbekcha (users.py:33), lekin extractErrorMessage 422 massivini umumiy matnga almashtiradi — foydalanuvchi sababni ko'rmaydi.

**Tekshiruv:** Frontend hali regex /[^a-zA-Z0-9]/ (ASCII) ishlatadi, backend hali Python isalnum() (Unicode-aware) — oʻ/gʻ apostrofi kabi belgilar frontendda o'tadi, serverda 422 qaytaradi. Ikkalasi ham o'zgarmagan.

**Tuzatish:** Tighten the server side to the same ASCII definition — `if not any(not c.isascii() or not c.isalnum() for c in v)` is wrong for the reverse reason, so prefer an explicit class: `if not re.search(r"[^A-Za-z0-9]", v)` in `validate_password_strength` — which makes both sides use one rule.

### `frontend/src/queries/mes.ts:33-58` — BUG · origin-08-29 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

getClientsQueryOptions and getEmployeesQueryOptions ask for one 100-row page and never paginate, while the page header renders the server's FULL count — past 100 rows the header and the table disagree and the extra rows are unreachable.

**Dalil:** limit: PAGE_SIZE,

**Repro:** (1) Server side, no DB: `cd backend && uv run python -c` compiling the read_employees query with SQLAlchemy/postgresql dialect -> `COUNT SQL has LIMIT? False` and rows SQL tail ` LIMIT 100 OFFSET 0`, i.e. count is the FULL population while rows are capped (clients.py:60-67 has the identical shape; config.py:70 MAX_PAGE_SIZE=100 == queries/mes.ts:17 PAGE_SIZE). (2) Client side, `node repro23.mjs` running the two real code shapes against a 150-row stub server: `clients: header "150 mijoz" | table rows = 100 -> 50 rows unreachable` vs `users: header "150" | table rows = 150 -> 0 unreachable`. getUsersQueryOptions (users.ts:16 `while (data.length < first.count)`) loops; getClientsQueryOptions/getEmployeesQueryOptions do not.

**Tekshiruv:** getClientsQueryOptions va getEmployeesQueryOptions hali bitta 100-qatorlik sahifa so'raydi (getUsersQueryOptions'dagi while (data.length < first.count) sikli yo'q), shu vaqtda sahifa header'i serverning to'liq count'ini ko'rsatadi.

**Tuzatish:** Do the same page loop as getUsersQueryOptions (or add explicit skip + TablePagination). Cheapest honest stopgap: show `data.data.length` in the header when it is below `data.count`, so the number never claims rows the table does not have.

### `frontend/src/routes/_layout/anketa.$orderId.tsx:146` — BUG · yangi sweep · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

Debounce effekti oldingi save.mutate hali tugamagan bo'lsa ham (save.isPending tekshirilmaydi) yangi mutate chaqiradi; har ikkalasi ham survey'ning TO'LIQ nusxasini yuboradi, shuning uchun tarmoqda javoblar chaqirilish tartibidan boshqacha kelsa, eskiroq snapshot keyinroq javob berib yangi tahrirlarni serverda ustidan yozib qo'yishi mumkin.

Repro: scratchpad/repro_anketa.mjs — real @tanstack/query-core 5.102.8 MutationObserver bilan effekt mantig'i ko'chirildi, A(v1) latency 60ms, B(v2) 10ms. Natija: `pendingAtSecondEdit: true, server: 'v1', client: 'v2', dirty: false, lost: true` — B onSuccess dirty=false qildi, A keyin kelib serverni v1 ga qaytardi, unmount flush ham ishlamaydi. Xuddi shu skriptda `scope: { id: 'survey' }` qo'shilganda: `server: 'v2', lost: false` (TanStack v5 scope bir xil id'li mutatsiyalarni ketma-ket bajaradi).

**Tekshiruv:** Debounce effekti (144-148 qatorlar) hali ham save.isPending ni tekshirmaydi — oldingi mutatsiya tugamasdan yangisi chaqirilishi mumkin, last-write-wins muammosi o'zgarmagan.

**Tuzatish:** Effekt ichida `if (save.isPending) return` guard qo'shish (yoki save.isPending ni dependency qilib, joriy mutatsiya tugagach qayta urinish), shunda bir vaqtda faqat bitta saqlash so'rovi tashqariga chiqadi.

### `frontend/src/routes/_layout/anketa.$orderId.tsx:79` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`canEdit` faqat rolni tekshiradi, buyurtma holatini emas — topshirilgan/bekor qilingan buyurtmada menejer to'liq tahrirlanadigan forma oladi, lekin har avtosaqlash 403 bilan qaytadi.

**Dalil:** const canEdit = isManager(currentUser)

Backend: _get_order'ni AsyncMock bilan patch qilib save_order_survey chaqirildi → DELIVERED va CANCELLED uchun 403 "Yakunlangan yoki bekor qilingan buyurtma anketasini o'zgartirib bo'lmaydi." Aniqlik: avtosaqlash effekti [survey,dirty] o'zgarganda ishlaydi, xato bo'lsa qayta urinmaydi — ya'ni 'har 1.5s oqim' emas, har tahrirda 1 ta 403 toast; beforeunload'dagi saqlash ham yiqiladi.

**Tekshiruv:** canEdit hali ham faqat rolga qaraydi, order.status ishtirok etmaydi. Backend (backend/app/api/routes/orders/survey.py:33) `if order.status in (OrderStatus.DELIVERED, OrderStatus.CANCELLED)` bilan 403 qaytaradi — menejer topshirilgan/bekor qilingan buyurtma anketasini ochsa avtosaqlash 403 toast oqimi beradi. Arzon, real UX bug.

**Tuzatish:** `canEdit` ga `&& order.status !== "delivered" && order.status !== "cancelled"` qo'shib (buyurtma yuklangandan keyin hisoblanadi), bunday holatda `RoomSummary` ko'rinishini va "Buyurtma yakunlangan — anketa faqat o'qish uchun" izohini ko'rsatish.

### `frontend/src/routes/_layout/orders/$orderId.tsx:201` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

customCount is read from the query cache with getQueryData() during render, which creates no subscription — the tab label's count is missing on first paint and never updates when the matrix loads or is edited.

Output: `after first paint tab = Maxsus (0) | parent renders: [0]` then `after matrix loads tab = Maxsus (0) | child: loaded 3 | parent renders: [0]`, with `cache now has parts: 3`. The parent never re-rendered; the label stayed stale. Fix verified in the same harness (repro4b.mjs, parent uses `useQuery({...KEY, enabled: false})`): `after matrix loads tab = Maxsus (2) | parent renders: [0,0,2]`.

### `frontend/src/routes/_layout/printers.tsx:202` — BUG · origin-08-29 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

The mebelix-agent.exe download link is the only place in the whole frontend that builds an API URL without API_BASE, so whenever the API lives on a different origin (the dev default http://localhost:8000) the link resolves against the SPA host and 404s.

**Dalil:** href="/api/v1/bridge-agents/download"

**Repro:** grep over frontend/src: `href="/api/...` matches exactly one line — printers.tsx:202; every other raw API URL uses API_BASE (utils.ts:119, used by main.tsx:17, useMesWs.ts:20, scxDir.ts:46, reports.tsx:51, RoomPhotos.tsx:90, $orderId.tsx:198). printers.tsx imports only `handleError` from @/utils (line 63). `node -e` URL resolution: dev SPA at :5173 -> `http://localhost:5173/api/v1/bridge-agents/download` while every other page goes to `http://localhost:8000/...`. vite.config.ts has NO dev proxy and there is no frontend/.env, so API_BASE falls back to http://localhost:8000 (utils.ts:120). Same break in docker-compose.local.yml:86 (frontend on :3000 built with VITE_API_URL=http://localhost:8000). Prod is NOT affected: ci.yml:106 builds with `VITE_API_URL=` and `"" ?? fallback === ""` (verified in node), so API_BASE="" and both forms resolve to the same same-origin URL.

**Tekshiruv:** Hali API_BASE import qilinmagan va prefiks qo'shilmagan; dev muhitida (VITE_API_URL != SPA host) havola SPA host'iga nisbatan hal qilinib 404 beradi.

**Tuzatish:** Import `API_BASE` from `@/utils` (printers.tsx already imports `handleError` from there) and write `href={`${API_BASE}/api/v1/bridge-agents/download`}`.

### `frontend/src/routes/_layout/reports.tsx:61` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

Excel eksporti oddiy `<a href>` navigatsiyasi — xato holatida (403/500/sessiya tugagan) brauzer SPA dan chiqib ketib xom JSON xatosini ekranga chiqaradi; hech qanday xato ishlovi yoki toast yo'q.

**Dalil:** <a href={exportUrl()} download>

**Repro:** Executed against the real ASGI app, no DB needed (`cd backend && uv run --with httpx python -c ...` driving app.main:app through httpx.ASGITransport): GET /api/v1/reports/productivity?format=xlsx with no cookie AND with an invalid `access_token` cookie both return `status: 401 | ct: application/json | disp: None | body: {"detail":"Tizimga kirilmagan."}` — a JSON body with no Content-Disposition, which is what the browser receives from `<a href download>` when the session has lapsed. `grep -n "handleError|onError|toast|useMutation|onClick" reports.tsx` -> zero matches, so nothing surfaces that response. `grep -rn interceptors frontend/src` -> the silent-refresh interceptor exists only on `client.instance` (main.tsx:26), which a plain anchor navigation never touches, while ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES=30 (config.py:35). 403 is much less likely than the claim implies: require_roles (deps.py:70) passes admin and superuser, so the page guard and the endpoint agree.

### `frontend/src/routes/_layout/work-centers.tsx:208` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

The auto-suggested work-center name counts existing machines on the post, so after any deletion or manual rename it proposes a name that already exists and the server rejects the pre-filled default.

**Repro:** Kod hozir 201-203 qatorda: `const used = machines.filter((m) => m.post_id === post.id).length` → `${post.name}-${used + 1}`. Repro (node, aynan shu mantiq): machines=[Arra-2] (Arra-1 o'chirilgan) → suggest=Arra-2, collides=true; backend `machines.py:_check_name` lower()-tenglik bilan 'allaqachon bor' deb rad etadi. Birinchi bo'sh suffiks varianti Arra-1 beradi.

**Tekshiruv:** Taklif nomi hali ham sanoq bilan quriladi — o'chirishdan keyin mavjud nomni taklif qilib, server pre-filled defaultni rad etadi. Faqat taklif (foydalanuvchi tahrirlaydi, xato toast chiqadi), lekin birinchi bo'sh suffiksni topish ~3 qatorlik arzon tuzatish.

**Tuzatish:** Pick the first free suffix instead of the count, e.g. increment `n` while `machines.some(m => m.name === `${post.name}-${n}`)`.

### `frontend/src/routes/_layout/work-centers.tsx:65` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

A failed `/posts` request is silently swallowed — only the machines query drives the error branch, so the page renders with a blank Post column and a broken sort order and no error is shown.

**Repro:** `grep useQuery|isError work-centers.tsx` → isError/refetch faqat machines (60-64), posts faqat `{ data: posts }` (65). Repro (node, 65-77 dagi sort/postById so'zma-so'z): posts=undefined, 3 ta machine → tartib faqat nom bo'yicha ['Altendorf arra','Biesse oval','Zebra kromka'], Post ustuni hammasi bo'sh, `disabled={!posts}` → Qo'shish tugmasi o'lik; QueryError chiqmaydi.

**Tekshiruv:** posts so'rovi yiqilsa hali ham jim yutiladi: isError faqat machines'dan olinadi, jadval Post ustunisiz va buzuq tartibda chiqadi, Qo'shish tugmasi o'lik qoladi, QueryError chiqmaydi. Kichik tuzatish, real jim degradatsiya.

**Tuzatish:** Combine both queries into the guard, e.g. `const isError = machinesQ.isError || postsQ.isError` and retry both in `onRetry`, so a posts outage shows `QueryError` instead of a silently degraded table.

### `frontend/src/routes/tablo.tsx:136-168` — BUG · origin-08-29 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

[false]`; (3) drill yopildi (rows.length O'ZGARMADI): effectRuns=1, `yangi tbody eski tbody bilan bir xilmi? false`, `observer hali eski tugunni kuzatyaptimi? true`, `eski tugun isConnected? false`, `YANGI tbody kuzatilyaptimi? false`; (4) rows.length 3->4: effectRuns=2, yangi observer jonli tbody da.

### `frontend/src/routes/tablo.tsx:968` — BUG · yangi sweep · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

TabloOrderDrill's `stall` badge is computed from `row?.idle_days`, but `row` becomes `undefined` both when the order genuinely never started (idle_days null) AND when the order has simply left the board (completed/moved out of PRODUCTION status while the drill was open, or matrix not yet cached). `stallLabel` treats `null`/`undefined` identically, returning "BOSHLANMAGAN" (not started) in both cases. The deadline badge right next to it is correctly gated with `row &&`, but the stall badge is not, so a just-finished order can flash a false "NOT STARTED" tag in the drill header — the exact opposite of the code's own comment at line 967-968 which says the context badges should simply disappear ("kontekst ko'rinmaydi") when `row` is missing.

**Dalil:** const stall = stallLabel(row?.idle_days)

**Repro:** Kod hozir ham shunday (969-970: `const row = matrix?.orders.find(...)`; `const stall = stallLabel(row?.idle_days)`; 1008 muddat belgisi `row &&` bilan to'silgan, 1027 `{stall && ...}` to'silmagan). Repro: scratchpad/repro_tablo.mjs — tablo.tsx dan HAQIQIY stallLabel matnini regex bilan kesib olib bajardi: `row: undefined -> stall: 'BOSHLANMAGAN', badgeRendered: true`; `row ? stallLabel(row.idle_days) : null` bilan `null`. Real yo'l: backend tablo.py:55 matritsa faqat `status == PRODUCTION`; oxirgi TMO skani statusni COMPLETED qiladi (tablo.py:22 izoh), WS `scan.*` hodisasi 323-satrda `['tablo']` ni invalidate qiladi — drill ochiq turganda row 60s polling'ni kutmasdan darhol yo'qoladi.

**Tekshiruv:** stall hisobi hali row mavjudligidan qat'i nazar chaqiriladi (968-qator), deadline belgisi esa 1006-qatorda `row &&` bilan to'silgan — nomuvofiqlik saqlanib qolgan.

**Tuzatish:** Gate the stall computation/render on `row` presence, e.g. `const stall = row ? stallLabel(row.idle_days) : null`, so a missing row hides the badge instead of showing a misleading "BOSHLANMAGAN".

### `frontend/src/routes/terminal.tsx:328-335` — BUG · yangi sweep · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

endSession mutatsiyasida onError yo'q — startShift, scanMutation, undoMutation hammasi xatoni toast.error bilan ko'rsatadi va playError() chaqiradi, lekin endSession xato bo'lsa (masalan tarmoq uzilsa) hech qanday belgi chiqmaydi: dialog ochiq qoladi (setEndFor(null) faqat onSuccess'da chaqiriladi), lekin operator nima uchun ekan bilmaydi — xato jim yutiladi.

Repro: scratchpad/repro_terminal.mjs — query-core MutationObserver, faqat onSettled + per-call onSuccess, mutationFn 'Network Error' tashlaydi, `.catch(noop)` (react-query useMutation.js:14 aynan shunday yutadi). ### `frontend/src/routes/terminal.tsx:346-353` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

The offline (E007) scan path is the only scan outcome that does not stamp `lastScanAtRef`, so the kiosk's "quiet time" gate can decide it is safe to auto-reload while the operator is actively scanning through a WS outage.

**Dalil:** onOffline: () => {       playError()       setLastScan({         kind: "error",         code: "E007",

**Repro:** onOffline (346-354) hali ham faqat playError+setLastScan(E007), `lastScanAtRef` yo'q; boshqa 4 yo'l (202,217,270,292) ref'ni qo'yadi; useTerminalScan.ts:78-79 `if (!online) { onOffline(); return }`. useKiosk.ts:65-74 tryReload har 30s `canReloadRef()` ni tekshiradi; terminal.tsx:125 gate = `Date.now()-lastScanAtRef.current > QUIET_MS(120s)`. Repro: oxirgi OK skan 130s oldin, hozir offline skan → canReload=true (reload bo'ladi); online skan → false.

**Tekshiruv:** onOffline callback hali ham lastScanAtRef.current ni yangilamaydi (boshqa hamma skan yo'llari — 202,217,270,292 — yangilaydi); useKioskShell (125-qator) shu ref bo'yicha 'tinchlik'ni o'lchaydi, ya'ni WS uzilganda faol skanlashda avto-reload xavfi qoladi.

**Tuzatish:** Add `lastScanAtRef.current = Date.now()` to the `onOffline` callback (and consider doing the same in `onBadge`, since a badge scan is also a scan attempt).

### `frontend/src/utils.ts:39` — TAKROR · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`extractErrorMessage` and `detailParts` implement the same six-branch error ladder in the same file, duplicating two user-facing strings verbatim and giving the same axios error two different Uzbek texts.

**Repro:** grep -n confirmed: 'Tarmoq xatosi — internet aloqasini tekshiring' literally duplicated at lines 42 and 76; ECONNABORTED gives two DIFFERENT texts — line 45 'So'rov vaqti tugadi — qayta urinib ko'ring' vs line 82 'Server javob bermadi — qayta urining'. Both funcs now share errorDetail()/holatXabari() helpers (partial dedup already done), but the two verbatim/conflicting strings the claim names are still there.

**Tekshiruv:** Ikki parallel zinapoya (extractErrorMessage 39-68, detailParts 72-104) hali ham turibdi: ikkita satr so'zma-so'z takror (42/76, 55/90), ECONNABORTED uchun esa ikki xil matn (45: 'So'rov vaqti tugadi', 82: 'Server javob bermadi'). Bitta ladder ustiga qurish drift'ni yopadi; refactor arzon.

**Tuzatish:** Implement `extractErrorMessage` on top of `detailParts` (e.g. return `message || code`, prefixing the code only for MES `{code,message}` envelopes) so there is one ladder and one string per condition.

**Tekshiruv:** useCurrentUser.ts bo'limi (docs/05:292) to'g'ri tuzatilgan — endi guard keshdan o'qishini ensureQueryData semantikasiga bog'laydi, staleTime'ga emas. Lekin queries/users.ts bo'limida (endi 318-qator) xuddi o'sha eski noto'g'ri jumla — staleTime guard'larning kesh o'qishini ta'minlaydi degan da'vo — hali ham bor.

## bridge-agent

### `bridge-agent/agent.py:1244` — BUG · origin-08-29 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`_restart_self()` va `_apply_update()` .bat faylni `encoding="ascii"` bilan yozadi — Windows profil nomi ASCII bo'lmasa (masalan `Oʻktam`, kirillcha nom) UnicodeEncodeError chiqadi va avto-yangilanish hamda tray'dagi «Qayta ulash» JIMGINA ishlamay qoladi.

**Dalil:** encoding="ascii",

**Repro:** Ikkala funksiyani AST bilan agent.py dan ajratib, soxta globals (CONFIG_DIR=tmpdir, sys.executable='C:\\Users\\Oʻktam\\MebelixAgent\\bridge-agent.exe') bilan bajardim: `python3 -c` (ast.parse + exec) → `_restart_self REPRO -> UnicodeEncodeError: 'ascii' codec can't encode character '\u02bb' in position 59` va `_apply_update REPRO -> UnicodeEncodeError: ... position 63`. Popen'gacha yetmaydi — write_text o'zi yiqiladi. Kontekst grepdan: `CONFIG_DIR = Path(os.environ.get("APPDATA", ...)) / "MebelixAgent"` (agent.py:252), ya'ni `_apply_update` bat matni HAR DOIM profil nomini o'z ichiga oladi (`new_exe = CONFIG_DIR / "mebelix-agent-new.exe"`, 1341). Jimlik ham tasdiqlandi: `self_update()` hamma narsani `except Exception: log.info(...)` ga o'raydi (1348-1349), `_repair_from_tray()` esa `_pair()` .env ni yozgandan keyin `_restart_self()` ni try/except SIZ chaqiradi (1270).

**Tekshiruv:** Both _restart_self (line 1244) and _apply_update (line 1290) still write the .bat file with encoding="ascii"; _repair_from_tray still calls _restart_self() unwrapped (no try/except around it) after _pair() re-writes the token.

**Tuzatish:** `encoding="ascii"` o'rniga `encoding="utf-8"` va bat'ning ikkinchi qatoriga `chcp 65001 >nul` qo'shish (mbcs/oem ham universal emas — tekshirildi). Kamida `_restart_self()` chaqiruvini try/except bilan o'rab, xatoni `_alert(..., error=True)` bilan ko'rsatish — aks holda operator token almashdi deb o'ylaydi.

### `bridge-agent/agent.py:780-801,914-950,1447-1454` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

Navbat fayli bilan ishlashda (local_drain/local_rewrite/local_enqueue) hech qanday xato ushlanmaydi; bitta OSError (Windows'da antivirus/backup faylni qulflaganda odatiy) `asyncio.gather` orqali butun agentni o'ldiradi, `__main__` esa faqat KeyboardInterrupt va SystemExit ni ushlaydi — oynasiz exe hech qanday xabar bermay jimgina o'ladi va chop etish to'xtaydi.

**Dalil:** except SystemExit as exc:

**Repro:** uv run python scratchpad/p08/r5_flusher.py: agent.py dan flusher() manbasini ast bilan ajratib (import qilib bo'lmaydi — modul darajasida _pair/_single_instance), local_drain ni PermissionError(13, 'used by another process') beradigan qilib gather(uplink(), flusher()) ni ishga tushirdim: ast tekshiruvi 'flusher ichida try/except: False'; asyncio.run(main()) OSError bilan chiqdi, uplink vazifasi bekor qilindi (CancelledError). agent.py:1467-1476 __main__ faqat KeyboardInterrupt/SystemExit ushlaydi; README:105 --noconsole bilan quriladi -> stderr yo'q, jimgina o'ladi.

**Tekshiruv:** local_drain/local_rewrite (780-801) flusher() ichida (914-950) hali try/except'siz chaqiriladi; __main__ (1448-1454) hali faqat KeyboardInterrupt va SystemExit'ni ushlaydi, umumiy except Exception yo'q. uplink (1132 atrofida) va log_shipper endi himoyalangan, lekin flusher himoyasiz qolgan — my_note aynan shuni tasdiqlaydi.

**Tuzatish:** flusher/uplink sikllarini `try/except Exception: log.exception(...); await asyncio.sleep(5)` bilan o'rash (vazifa o'lmasin) va `__main__` ga `except Exception` qo'shib `_alert(..., error=True)` chiqarish — aks holda nosozlik faqat agent.log da qoladi va log_shipper ham o'lgani uchun serverga yetmaydi.

## infra

### `.dockerignore:4` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`**/.env` naqshi faqat aynan `.env` nomini tutadi — `.env.server` kabi variantlar build kontekstiga uzatiladi (frontend'ning o'z `.dockerignore`'i esa `.env.*` ni ham yopadi).

**Repro:** `.dockerignore` da hali `**/.env` (4-qator), `.env.*` yo'q; `frontend/.dockerignore` da esa `.env` + `.env.*` bor. Docker patternmatcher simulyatsiyasi (Go Match + ** → regex, docker ishlatilmadi): `.env`/`backend/.env` → tutiladi; `.env.server`/`backend/.env.server` → tutilMAYDI; `**/.env.*` bo'lsa tutiladi. Dockerfile:36 `COPY backend /app/backend` — backend/.env.* paydo bo'lsa image'ga tushadi. Hozir repo'da faqat `./.env` va `./.env.example` bor.

**Tekshiruv:** Naqsh o'zgarmagan — .env.* variantlari yopilmaydi (frontend/.dockerignore da yopiladi). Konkret .env.server fayli endi yo'q, lekin backend/.env.* paydo bo'lsa image ichiga COPY bo'lardi (Dockerfile backend/ ni to'liq ko'chiradi). Bir qatorlik himoya, docs/07:493 'secretlar image'ga tushmaydi' da'vosiga moslash uchun arzon.

**Tuzatish:** `.dockerignore` ga `**/.env.*` qatorini qo'shib `!**/.env.example` istisnosini berish (yoki `.env.example` ni baribir kerak emas — backend Dockerfile faqat `backend`/`scripts` ni COPY qiladi), va `.gitignore` ga `.env.*` + `!.env.example` qo'shish.

### `.gitignore:10-11` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

Only `.env` and `.env.local` are ignored, not `.env.*` — the repo currently has an untracked, un-ignored `.env.server` sitting in the root, one `git add .` away from committing server secrets. The docs claim all ".env fayllar" are ignored.

**Dalil:** .env .env.local

**Repro:** `git check-ignore -q`: .env → yes, .env.local → yes, backend/.env → yes, .env.server → NO, .env.prod → NO, backend/.env.server → NO. `ls .env.server` — fayl endi yo'q; git tarixida hech qachon .env varianti commit qilinmagan (faqat .env.example). docs/07:508 esa '.env fayllar (secretlar repoga tushmaydi)' deb keng da'vo qiladi.

**Tekshiruv:** Hali ham faqat .env va .env.local ignore qilinadi, .env.* yo'q — naqsh teshigi (masalan .env.server) turibdi. Bir qatorlik secret-himoya tuzatishi arzon.

**Tuzatish:** Replace the two lines with `.env` + `.env.*` + `!.env.example` (keeping the example tracked). Mirror the same pattern in `.dockerignore` so `.env.server` never reaches the backend build context either.

### `Makefile:43-44 & frontend/package.json:10` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`make frontend-lint` ("Run frontend linting checks") is not a check — `npm run lint` is `biome check --write --unsafe`, which applies unsafe autofixes to source and cannot fail on anything biome can rewrite.

**Dalil:** frontend-lint: ## Run frontend linting checks 	cd frontend && npm run lint

**Repro:** Makefile:46 hali `npm run lint` = package.json:10 `biome check --write --unsafe`. Scratchpad'da frontend/biome.json + formatlanmagan x.ts bilan: `biome ci ./` → exit 1 ("File content differs"); `biome check --write --unsafe ...` → exit 0, "Fixed 1 file", fayl qayta yozildi; keyin `biome ci` exit 0. CI (ci.yml:77) `npx biome ci .` ishlatadi.

**Tekshiruv:** npm run lint hali ham 'biome check --write --unsafe ...' (package.json:10) — 'linting checks' deb atalgan target manbani qayta yozadi, CI esa 'npx biome ci .' ishlatadi (nomuvofiqlik davom etmoqda).

**Tuzatish:** Point `frontend-lint` at the same command CI uses (`cd frontend && npx biome ci .`), or add a separate `frontend-format` target and reserve `frontend-lint` for the check-only invocation.

### `Makefile:56-58` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`make format` runs ruff from the repo root, so it reformats files no linter in the project owns — `docs/*.md` python blocks and `bridge-agent/agent.py` (at ruff's default line-length 88, since no ruff config exists outside backend/). The docs document the damage and ask the developer to hand-revert it instead of fixing the target.

**Dalil:** format: ## Run code formatting (ruff) 	uv --project backend run ruff format . 	uv --project backend run ruff check . --fix

**Repro:** Makefile:59-60 hali `uv --project backend run ruff format .` (ildizdan). Check-only repro: `uv --project backend run ruff format --check .` (ruff 0.16.5) → exit 1, backend/ tashqarisida 4 fayl qayta yozilardi: bridge-agent/agent.py (444: line-length 88 bo'yicha bo'lish), docs/02, docs/03, docs/04 .md python bloklari. `cd backend && ruff format --check app` → 139 files already formatted, exit 0. docs/07:418 da 'git diff ni ko'rib tashlab yuborish' workaround'i hali turibdi.

**Tekshiruv:** make format hali ham repo ildizidan '.' ga ishlaydi — docs/*.md va bridge-agent/agent.py hech bir linter talab qilmaydigan qoidalar bilan qayta yoziladi. backend app ga toraytirish arzon.

**Tuzatish:** Scope the target the same way `lint`, CI and the pre-commit hooks are scoped: `cd backend && uv run ruff format app && uv run ruff check app --fix`. Then delete the "inspect git diff afterwards" workaround from docs/07-infra-devops.md:408.

### `docker-compose.prod.yml:56-62 | .env.example:60-66` — HARDCODE · yangi sweep · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

XFF ishonch diapazoni ikki joyda mustaqil belgilangan: .env dagi FORWARDED_ALLOW_IPS (backend/app/core/setup.py:174 da ProxyHeadersMiddleware shuni o'qiydi) va bu yerda gunicorn CLI flagiga qattiq yozilgan '172.16.0.0/12'. Izoh nega flag sifatida berilganini tushuntiradi (gunicorn FORWARDED_ALLOW_IPS env'ini o'zi ham o'qiydi va pydantic JSON formatida yiqiladi), lekin ikki qiymatning sinxron turishi hech narsa bilan ta'minlanmagan.

**Dalil:** command: bash -c "/app/scripts/prestart.sh && gunicorn app.main:app -w ${GUNICORN_WORKERS:-4} -k uvicorn.workers.UvicornWorker -b 0.0.0.0:8000 --forwarded-allow-ips '172.16.0.0/12'"

**Repro:** grep: .env.example:67 FORWARDED_ALLOW_IPS=["172.16.0.0/12"], compose:62 --forwarded-allow-ips '172.16.0.0/12' qattiq yozilgan, docs/07:161 ikkalasini alohida-alohida hujjatlab 'ikkalasi ham 172.16.0.0/12' deydi — demak duplikatsiya haqiqiy va allaqachon qisman hujjatlangan, lekin sinxronlashni ta'minlovchi mexanizm yo'q.

**Tekshiruv:** Ikkala joyda (.env.example:66 va compose:62) hali mustaqil '172.16.0.0/12' qattiq yozilgan, docs/07 §160 ikki qatlamni tushuntiradi lekin o'zaro havola yo'q. Asl arzon taklif (bir qatorlik izoh-havola) hali qo'llanmagan; sev past qoladi.

**Tuzatish:** .env.example'dagi izohga ikkinchi joy (docker-compose.prod.yml command) borligini va uni ham qo'lda yangilash kerakligini qo'shib yozing, yoki compose'da o'z nomli o'zgaruvchi (masalan GUNICORN_FORWARDED_ALLOW_IPS) kiritib ${...:-172.16.0.0/12} shaklida referenslang — shunda bitta joyni o'zgartirish yetarli bo'ladi.

### `scripts/deploy.sh:102` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

The auto-rollback path re-runs `up -d` with the old image TAG but never re-runs `caddy reload`, so a rolled-back stack keeps the new Caddyfile that was loaded on the way in — the rollback restores code but not proxy config.

**Repro:** `grep -n 'caddy reload' scripts/deploy.sh` → faqat 78-qator (smoke'dan OLDIN); rollback bloki 100-107 da faqat `TAG="$STABLE" $COMPOSE up -d`. caddy image `caddy:2-alpine` (TAG'ga bog'liq emas) → up -d uni qayta yaratmaydi, yangi konfig xotirada qoladi; rsync --delete (ci.yml) eski Caddyfile'ni serverda qoldirmaydi, ya'ni reload qo'shsa ham qaytaradigan narsa yo'q.

**Tekshiruv:** Rollback hali ham faqat 'up -d' — caddy reload yo'q, diskda esa rsync qo'ygan YANGI Caddyfile turadi; sintaktik to'g'ri lekin semantik buzuq konfig smoke'ni yiqitsa rollback tuzata olmaydi va 'ROLLBACK SMOKE HAM YIQILDI' ga tushadi. Kamida rollback xabariga Caddyfile qaytarilmasligini yozish arzon; CI validate faqat sintaksisni tutadi.

**Tuzatish:** Either add the same `caddy reload` after the rollback `up -d` (with `git`/backup copy of the previous Caddyfile), or move the reload after a successful smoke so a failed deploy never leaves a half-applied proxy config. At minimum, say in the rollback message that Caddyfile/compose/scripts are NOT rolled back.

### `scripts/deploy.sh:19` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`CADDY_HTTP_BIND` is read from `.env` without stripping surrounding quotes, while `SITE_ADDRESS` two lines below is explicitly de-quoted — a quoted bind value produces a malformed curl URL, so smoke fails 30 times and triggers a false auto-rollback of a healthy deploy.

**Repro:** deploy.sh:19-43 fragmentini fake .env bilan source qildim. `CADDY_HTTP_BIND="127.0.0.1:8200"` → SMOKE_URL=[http://"127.0.0.1:8200"/api/v1/ready], curl exit=3 (malformed URL) http_code=000 → smoke() 30x5s dan keyin rollback. Qo'shtirnoqsiz → to'g'ri URL (exit 7 = server yo'q, kutilgan). SITE (32-33) tozalanadi, BIND tozalanmaydi; backup.sh:27 da tayyor env_get helper bor.

**Tekshiruv:** BIND hali ham qo'shtirnoqdan tozalanmaydi, ikkita pastdagi SITE esa tozalanadi (SITE="${SITE#\"}"). Qo'shtirnoqlangan qiymat sog' deploy'ni 30x5s smoke'dan keyin yolg'ondan rollback qiladi. .env.example da qo'shtirnoq konventsiyasi bor (VITE_API_URL) — moslash bir-ikki qator.

**Tuzatish:** Extract one `env_get` helper used by both values that strips surrounding quotes (and, unlike scripts/backup.sh:27, strips only a leading `#` comment, not every `#`), then use it for `CADDY_HTTP_BIND` and `SITE_ADDRESS` alike.

### `scripts/setup.sh:1-3 & scripts/generate-client.sh:1-6` — BUG · eski-2026-08-26 · ✅ BAJARIB ISBOTLANGAN

`setup.sh` and `generate-client.sh` assume the caller's cwd is the repo root, while their two siblings `deploy.sh` and `backup.sh` both normalise cwd first. Run either of the first two directly (they are mode 100755, so `./scripts/setup.sh` from inside `scripts/` is a natural thing to do) and every relative path resolves wrong.

**Dalil:** #!/bin/bash  set -e

**Repro:** setup.sh va generate-client.sh da hali `cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."` yo'q (deploy.sh:12, backup.sh:16 da bor). `cd scripts && [ -f .env ]` → absent; `cp .env.example ...` → 'cannot stat' exit 1 (set -e ostida shu yerda o'ladi); `cd scripts && bash -c 'set -e; cd backend'` → 'No such file or directory' exit 1. Makefile ikkalasini ildizdan `./scripts/...` deb chaqiradi, docs/08:170 esa `bash scripts/generate-client.sh` deydi — ildizdan.

**Tekshiruv:** setup.sh da ham, generate-client.sh da ham hali `cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."` yo'q (deploy.sh/backup.sh singari); generate-client.sh ham to'g'ridan cd backend qiladi, root'ga normalizatsiya qilmasdan.

**Tuzatish:** Add `cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."` right after `set -e` in both scripts, matching deploy.sh:12 and backup.sh:16.  ---

## Deploy-xavfsizlik tahlili (2026-09-02, 3 hakam + repro)

- **Avto-rollback migratsiyali deploy'da ISHLAMAYDI** (bajarib isbotlangan): yangi image prestart'i DB'ni yangi revision'ga ko'taradi; smoke yiqilsa `deploy.sh` eski image'ni ko'taradi, uning `alembic upgrade head` i noma'lum revision'da `Can't locate revision` bilan yiqiladi (`prestart.sh` `set -e`), api `restart: always` bilan crash loop. `docs/07` dagi «migratsiyalar additiv bo'lsa rollback xavfsiz» taxmini noto'g'ri. Qo'lda tiklash: `TAG=<yangi> docker compose -f docker-compose.prod.yml run --rm api python -m alembic downgrade <eski>` (yoki `stamp <eski>`), so'ng `TAG=<stable> bash scripts/deploy.sh`. **Tuzatildi (shu kun):** `deploy.sh` rollback'i eski image'ni `SKIP_MIGRATIONS=1` bilan ko'taradi (`prestart.sh` bayrog'i, compose env), `stamp` YO'Q; additiv bo'lmagan migratsiya uchun `ROLLBACK_DOWNGRADE=1` — yangi image bilan `alembic downgrade <eski head>`. Prestart'ning o'zida yiqilish (migratsiya xatosi) ham yopildi — `up -d` exit 1 bersa skript smoke/rollback'ga davom etadi. Qoida docs/07 da.
- Downtime ~1 daqiqa (api/worker/frontend almashadi; db/redis/caddy joyida), WS qayta ulanadi, arq navbati Redis'da qoladi, kiosklar `useKiosk` ETag bilan xavfsiz paytda o'zi reload qiladi. Deploy oynasi: Toshkent 18:45 dan keyin.
- Eski SPA bundle o'chirilgan endpoint/maydonlarni ishlatmaydi (origin/main'da grep); bridge-agent v1.4.5 versiyasi o'zgarmagan — self-update bo'lmaydi; paket yangilanishlarida breaking o'zgarish yo'q.

## Metod

14 workflow, ~420 subagent chaqiruvi, ~27M token (2026-09-02, ikki bosqich: ertalab eski lokal daraxtda, tushdan keyin `origin/main` bazasida qayta):

| Bosqich | Agent | Model | Token |
| --- | ---: | --- | ---: |
| eski 804 topilma triaji (eski daraxt) | 58 | fable | 5.2M |
| docs/README/CLAUDE.md ↔ kod drift + adversarial rad | 68 | fable | 3.9M |
| yangi sweep backend+infra / frontend (finder + verify) | 91 | sonnet finder / fable verify | 5.2M |
| hujjat driftlarini tuzatish + eski drift qayta tekshiruv + diff hakam | 63 | sonnet muharrir / fable hakam | 3.7M |
| o'z diff'imni adversarial tekshirish + 40 yuqori band isboti | 12 | fable | 0.9M |
| tozalash bandlarini qo'llash (frontend) + hakam | 14 | sonnet/opus muharrir / fable hakam | 1.1M |
| 187 o'rta/past band isboti (repro bilan) + nazorat | 37 | fable / sonnet | 2.7M |
| **origin/main bazasida qayta triaj** (560 + 155 band) | 42 | fable (yuqori) / sonnet | 3.8M |
| yangi bazada 100 isbotsiz band isboti | 17 | opus | 1.4M |
| yakuniy diff hakamlari | 3 | fable | 0.3M |

Modelsiz (skript bilan) bajarilgan qadamlar: audit parse (804/804 va 768/768), chunk bo'lish, `grep -F` iqtibos darvozasi (har verdikt), origin ↔ lokal anchor tekshiruvi (101 tuzatish), sintez. Barcha darvozalar yashil: `ruff check --no-fix`, `ruff format --check`, `mypy`, `pre-commit`, `biome ci`, `tsc`, `vite build`, `make frontend-gen`. **Yuritilmagani:** `make check-migrations` — lokalda DB yo'q (CI toza Postgres'da yuritadi). `ix_pachka_order_id` drop migratsiyasi tayyorlangan, lekin deploy-xavfsizlik tahlili (pastda) sababli bu deploy'dan chiqarildi — TODO'da.

Sabzi: lokal `main` origin'dan 16 commit orqada edi (2026-08-27..31 da boshqa sessiya 804 topilmani re-verdict qilib, cleanup va 11 defektni tuzatgan). Ertalabki 104 faylli ish 90 faylda ustma-ust chiqdi; origin'da YO'Q 39 tuzatish qayta qo'llandi (patch: `~/.claude/projects/.../mine-2026-09-02.patch`).
